<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-50574968" data-id="50574968" data-tags="Science" data-score="2907" data-comments="2089" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50574968" data-time="1795184181">
<a class="thumb" href="https://alpo.ge/remark.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=alpo.ge&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>alpo.ge</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://alpo.ge/remark.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Levent Alpöge claims proof of the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>Levent Alpöge 声称证明了广义黎曼猜想</span></a><span class="dom">alpo.ge</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Levent Alpöge claims to have found a proof of the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis.</span><span class='zh'>Levent Alpöge 声称找到了广义黎曼猜想的证明。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Levent Alpöge claims to have found a proof of the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis. The claim is presented in a PDF.</span><span class='zh'>Levent Alpöge 声称找到了广义黎曼猜想的证明。相关论证以 PDF 形式发布。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>2907</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50574968' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2089</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50579568" data-id="50579568" data-tags="Science" data-score="2393" data-comments="1160" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50579568" data-time="1795199594">
<a class="thumb" href="https://math216.wordpress.com/2026/11/20/hurried-announcement-alessandro-masullo-grh/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=math216.wordpress.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>math216.wordpress.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://math216.wordpress.com/2026/11/20/hurried-announcement-alessandro-masullo-grh/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hurried Announcement: Alessandro Masullo GRH</span><span class='zh'>仓促公告：Alessandro Masullo 的广义黎曼猜想证明</span></a><span class="dom">math216.wordpress.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Alessandro Masullo has proposed a proof of the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis.</span><span class='zh'>Alessandro Masullo 提出了一份广义黎曼猜想的证明。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Alessandro Masullo has proposed a proof of the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis. Brian Conrad has reviewed it closely for months, provided extensive feedback, and considers the draft to be looking good so far. The authors had hoped to refine it at Stanford before announcing it, but decided to release the current draft so others could check and formalize it.</span><span class='zh'>Alessandro Masullo 提出了一份广义黎曼猜想的证明。Brian Conrad 已经对其进行了数月的仔细审阅，并提供了大量反馈，目前认为证明草稿看起来不错。原计划是在斯坦福进一步完善后再公布，但现在决定先发布当前版本，供其他人检查和形式化。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>4.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>2393</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50579568' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1160</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50522980" data-id="50522980" data-tags="Education" data-score="2283" data-comments="308" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50522980" data-time="1794861816">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cs.cmu.edu/learn/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Carnegie Mellon is putting computer science courses on YouTube for free</span><span class='zh'>卡内基梅隆大学将计算机科学课程免费放到 YouTube</span></a><span class="dom">cs.cmu.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Carnegie Mellon’s Kenneth C. Griffin School of Computer Science is publishing free lectures on YouTube.</span><span class='zh'>卡内基梅隆大学肯尼斯·C·格里芬计算机科学学院正在 YouTube 上发布免费课程讲座。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Carnegie Mellon’s Kenneth C. Griffin School of Computer Science is publishing free lectures on YouTube. Self-paced courseware is planned as the next step. The release gives learners access to the school’s computer science material without charge.</span><span class='zh'>卡内基梅隆大学肯尼斯·C·格里芬计算机科学学院正在 YouTube 上发布免费课程讲座。下一步计划推出可自主学习的课程材料。这将让学习者免费接触该学院的计算机科学内容。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.spacex.com/launches/starship-flight-15" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SpaceX catches the Starship to end Flight 15</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 在第 15 次飞行结束时接住星舰</span></a><span class="dom">spacex.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SpaceX&#x27;s Starship Flight 15 ended with the vehicle being caught by the launch system.</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 的星舰第 15 次飞行以发射系统接住飞行器告终。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SpaceX&#x27;s Starship Flight 15 ended with the vehicle being caught by the launch system. The available material provides no further details about the flight or the catch.</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 的星舰第 15 次飞行以发射系统接住飞行器告终。现有信息未提供这次飞行或捕获过程的更多细节。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.wiz.io/blog/nginx-http2-rce-vulnerabilities" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.datocms-assets.com/75231/1794936719-img_7875.png?fm=webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='wiz.io' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wiz.io/blog/nginx-http2-rce-vulnerabilities" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Unauthenticated remote code execution in Nginx</span><span class='zh'>Nginx 中存在无需认证的远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">wiz.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nginx has been reported to contain unauthenticated remote code execution vulnerabilities.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Nginx 存在无需认证即可触发的远程代码执行漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nginx has been reported to contain unauthenticated remote code execution vulnerabilities. The supplied records identify two related CVE entries, CVE-2026-123633 and CVE-2026-123007.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Nginx 存在无需认证即可触发的远程代码执行漏洞。相关记录列出了两个 CVE 编号：CVE-2026-123633 和 CVE-2026-123007。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>1483</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50547374' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>637</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50581260" data-id="50581260" data-tags="AI|Open Source" data-score="1456" data-comments="293" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50581260" data-time="1795205539">
<a class="thumb" href="https://thinkingmachines.ai/news/inkling-2-mini/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://thinkingmachines.ai/news/inkling-2-mini/images/cover-social.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='thinkingmachines.ai' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thinkingmachines.ai/news/inkling-2-mini/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Inkling 2 Mini</span><span class='zh'>Inkling 2 Mini</span></a><span class="dom">thinkingmachines.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Thinking Machines has released Inkling 2 Mini, an open-weights model small enough to run on a laptop.</span><span class='zh'>Thinking Machines 发布了 Inkling 2 Mini，这是一款小到可以在笔记本电脑上运行的开放权重模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Thinking Machines has released Inkling 2 Mini, an open-weights model small enough to run on a laptop. It is a looped transformer that runs its middle layers three times per token, allowing it to reason more and use fewer tokens. The model reportedly leads open-weights models under 100 billion total parameters on DeepSWE v1.1 and Vals Index v2.2, while also running faster than similarly performing models.</span><span class='zh'>Thinking Machines 发布了 Inkling 2 Mini，这是一款小到可以在笔记本电脑上运行的开放权重模型。它采用循环 Transformer，每个 token 将中间层运行三次，从而进行更多推理并减少 token 使用量。据称，在总参数量低于 1000 亿的开放权重模型中，它在 DeepSWE v1.1 和 Vals Index v2.2 上处于领先，同时运行速度也快于性能相近的模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>1456</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50581260' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>293</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50582069" data-id="50582069" data-tags="Health" data-score="1342" data-comments="300" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50582069" data-time="1795208592">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.theguardian.com/society/2026/nov/20/pill-pancreatic-cancer-breakthrough-daraxonrasib" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/d7bac7cca6890657580a396f9255cf7d7b3d2874/1250_0_6250_5000/master/6250.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=e108196e90ed29a731e8a7ec890173bf' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='theguardian.com' data-c='#4ade80' data-tag='Health'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/society/2026/nov/20/pill-pancreatic-cancer-breakthrough-daraxonrasib" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pill to treat pancreatic cancer hailed as &#x27;biggest breakthrough in decades&#x27;</span><span class='zh'>治疗胰腺癌的药片被誉为“数十年来最大突破”</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In a trial of daraxonrasib, the cancer became undetectable in one in 12 patients with advanced pancreatic cancer.</span><span class='zh'>在 daraxonrasib 的一项试验中，12 名晚期胰腺癌患者中有 1 人的癌症变得无法检测到。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In a trial of daraxonrasib, the cancer became undetectable in one in 12 patients with advanced pancreatic cancer. Tumors stopped growing in nearly all of the 61 participants, whose cancer had spread to other parts of the body. The results are being hailed as a major advance in pancreatic cancer treatment.</span><span class='zh'>在 daraxonrasib 的一项试验中，12 名晚期胰腺癌患者中有 1 人的癌症变得无法检测到。61 名参与者的肿瘤几乎全部停止生长，这些患者的癌症此前已扩散到身体其他部位。这些结果被誉为胰腺癌治疗领域的重大进展。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">09</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/11/17/upshot/school-phone-bans-study-scores.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>New Evidence Shows School Phone Bans Are Gradually Improving Test Scores</span><span class='zh'>新证据显示，学校禁用手机正逐步提高考试成绩</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A large national analysis comparing students’ scores before and after cellphone bans found modest academic improvements.</span><span class='zh'>一项比较学生禁用手机前后成绩的大型全国性分析发现，学业表现出现小幅改善。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A large national analysis comparing students’ scores before and after cellphone bans found modest academic improvements. Schools with strict policies requiring students to lock phones in pouches for the entire school day saw math and reading gains equivalent to roughly one or two additional weeks of schooling per year. The study was published by Harvard’s Center for Education Policy Research and is the first national study to track individual students’ growth in this way.</span><span class='zh'>一项比较学生禁用手机前后成绩的大型全国性分析发现，学业表现出现小幅改善。要求学生在整个上课日将手机锁入袋中的学校，数学和阅读成绩提升幅度相当于每年额外增加约一至两周的学习。该研究由哈佛教育政策研究中心发布，是首项以这种方式追踪个体学生成长的全国性研究。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://leanprover-community.github.io/blog/posts/riemann-hypothesis/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lean verification of the Riemann Hypothesis</span><span class='zh'>用 Lean 验证黎曼猜想——联合声明</span></a><span class="dom">leanprover-community.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Levent Alpöge released a Lean library containing a proof of the Riemann Hypothesis on November 20, 2026.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年 11 月 20 日，Levent Alpöge 发布了一个包含黎曼猜想证明的 Lean 库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Levent Alpöge released a Lean library containing a proof of the Riemann Hypothesis on November 20, 2026. The library comprises 17 million lines of code. The Lean and Mathlib communities issued a joint statement endorsing the verification, with support from the Lean FRO and the Mathlib Initiative.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年 11 月 20 日，Levent Alpöge 发布了一个包含黎曼猜想证明的 Lean 库。该库包含 1700 万行代码。Lean 和 Mathlib 社区随后发表联合声明，Lean FRO 与 Mathlib Initiative 也表示支持这项验证工作。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">11</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.caranddriver.com/news/a74490092/toyota-nissan-honda-standardize-parts-lower-costs/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Toyota, Nissan, and Honda to standardize parts to lower costs</span><span class='zh'>丰田、日产和本田将统一零部件以降低成本</span></a><span class="dom">caranddriver.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Toyota, Nissan, and Honda have agreed to standardize vehicle parts to reduce production costs as Japanese automakers compete with China’s growing automotive sector.</span><span class='zh'>丰田、日产和本田已同意统一汽车零部件标准，以降低生产成本，应对中国汽车产业的增长。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Toyota, Nissan, and Honda have agreed to standardize vehicle parts to reduce production costs as Japanese automakers compete with China’s growing automotive sector. The initial effort will unify quality criteria for steel, protocols for plastics and resins, and color coding for wiring harnesses. The Japan Automobile Manufacturers Association plans to expand standardization to more components across the vehicle, focusing first on parts that are largely invisible to consumers and represent significant costs.</span><span class='zh'>丰田、日产和本田已同意统一汽车零部件标准，以降低生产成本，应对中国汽车产业的增长。首阶段将统一钢材质量标准、塑料和树脂的处理规范，以及线束的颜色编码。日本汽车工业协会计划未来将标准化范围扩大到更多整车零部件，首先选择消费者通常看不到且成本占比较高的部件。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1194</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50596133' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>681</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50538240" data-id="50538240" data-tags="Security" data-score="1176" data-comments="329" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50538240" data-time="1794949888">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://calif.io/java4shell/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Java4Shell: RCE in OpenJDK when processing Base64</span><span class='zh'>Java4Shell：处理 Base64 时 OpenJDK 中的远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">calif.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Calif disclosed Java4Shell, tracked as CVE-2026-114938, a memory-corruption vulnerability in OpenJDK&#x27;s Base64 decoder.</span><span class='zh'>Calif 披露了 Java4Shell（编号 CVE-2026-114938），这是 OpenJDK Base64 解码器中的内存破坏漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Calif disclosed Java4Shell, tracked as CVE-2026-114938, a memory-corruption vulnerability in OpenJDK&#x27;s Base64 decoder. OpenJDK is described as the most popular Java runtime. The vulnerability has a CVSS 4.0 score of 9.2 out of 10, rated Critical. Its severity could make affected OpenJDK deployments a high-priority patching target.</span><span class='zh'>Calif 披露了 Java4Shell（编号 CVE-2026-114938），这是 OpenJDK Base64 解码器中的内存破坏漏洞。OpenJDK 被称为最流行的 Java 运行时。该漏洞在 CVSS 4.0 中获得 9.2 分（满分 10 分），评级为“严重”。这一评级意味着受影响的 OpenJDK 部署可能需要优先修补。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/introducing-gemini-4-neon/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Neon</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Neon</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google has introduced Gemini 4 Neon, a model aimed at complex coding, knowledge work, and scientific workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Google 推出了 Gemini 4 Neon，面向复杂编程、知识工作和科学工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google has introduced Gemini 4 Neon, a model aimed at complex coding, knowledge work, and scientific workflows. The company says it offers frontier-level intelligence with fast end-to-end task completion at a lower cost. The model is presented by Google DeepMind’s senior vice president and chief AI architect.</span><span class='zh'>Google 推出了 Gemini 4 Neon，面向复杂编程、知识工作和科学工作流。Google 表示，该模型以更低成本提供前沿级智能，并能快速完成端到端任务。该模型由 Google DeepMind 高级副总裁兼首席 AI 架构师发布。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="http://groundworkcollaborative.org/news/amazons-decoy-deals-groundwork-investigation-reveals-nearly-1-in-3-black-friday-and-cyber-monday-deals-advertised-by-amazon-offered-no-savings-to-shoppers-nearly-1-in-10-w/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Many of Amazon’s Black Friday and Cyber Monday “deals” offered no savings</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊许多黑色星期五和网络星期一“优惠”并没有省钱</span></a><span class="dom">groundworkcollaborative.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An investigation found that many Black Friday and Cyber Monday deals advertised by Amazon did not provide actual savings.</span><span class='zh'>一项调查发现，亚马逊宣传的许多黑色星期五和网络星期一优惠实际上并没有带来节省。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An investigation found that many Black Friday and Cyber Monday deals advertised by Amazon did not provide actual savings. The deals were presented as discounts even though shoppers often paid no less than usual.</span><span class='zh'>一项调查发现，亚马逊宣传的许多黑色星期五和网络星期一优惠实际上并没有带来节省。这些商品虽然以折扣形式展示，消费者实际支付的价格却往往并未降低。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.harrison.page/garage-door-spam/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Garage Door Spam</span><span class='zh'>车库门垃圾通知</span></a><span class="dom">blog.harrison.page</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A garage door opener initially sent notifications whenever the door opened or closed, which made unexpected openings seem meaningful when the owner was away.</span><span class='zh'>一款车库门开启器最初会在车门开关时发送通知，因此人在外出时，异常开门会显得格外重要。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A garage door opener initially sent notifications whenever the door opened or closed, which made unexpected openings seem meaningful when the owner was away. The owner continued using the myQ app despite its advertisements because the notifications appeared trustworthy.</span><span class='zh'>一款车库门开启器最初会在车门开关时发送通知，因此人在外出时，异常开门会显得格外重要。尽管 myQ 应用广告很多，用户仍因信任这些通知而继续使用它。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://betterdatacenterproject.com/2026/11/16/how-ai-is-powered-a-breakdown-of-byop-and-grid-power/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://i0.wp.com/betterdatacenterproject.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/anthropic-total-co2.png?fit=1200%2C1065&amp;ssl=1' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='betterdatacenterproject.com' data-c='#eab308' data-tag='Energy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://betterdatacenterproject.com/2026/11/16/how-ai-is-powered-a-breakdown-of-byop-and-grid-power/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>87% of the Anthropic power will come from gas and 11% from coal in 2028</span><span class='zh'>2028 年 Anthropic 的电力将有 87% 来自天然气、11% 来自煤炭</span></a><span class="dom">betterdatacenterproject.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A case study estimates that Anthropic could emit 32 million metric tons of carbon dioxide in 2028.</span><span class='zh'>一项案例研究估计，Anthropic 在 2028 年可能排放 3,200 万吨二氧化碳。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A case study estimates that Anthropic could emit 32 million metric tons of carbon dioxide in 2028. It examines the company’s total power use and the role of dedicated power generation for AI data centers, noting that planned bring-your-own-power gas capacity is far more common than renewable generation. The study also identifies another 200 GW of new fossil-fuel power plants being built on the grid, partly to meet data-center demand.</span><span class='zh'>一项案例研究估计，Anthropic 在 2028 年可能排放 3,200 万吨二氧化碳。研究考察了该公司的总用电量，以及 AI 数据中心专用发电的作用，并指出已规划的“自带电力”项目中，天然气发电远多于可再生能源。研究还指出，为满足包括数据中心在内的增长需求，电网上另有 200 吉瓦新的化石燃料发电厂正在建设。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/quad9-withdrawal-from-france/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Quad9&#x27;s withdrawal from France: A canary in the coal mine</span><span class='zh'>Quad9 退出法国：煤矿里的金丝雀</span></a><span class="dom">blog.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Swiss nonprofit Quad9 said it will withdraw its free public DNS resolver from France after months of litigation.</span><span class='zh'>瑞士非营利组织 Quad9 表示，将在经历数月诉讼后退出法国免费公共 DNS 解析服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Swiss nonprofit Quad9 said it will withdraw its free public DNS resolver from France after months of litigation. A French court imposed daily financial penalties because Quad9 did not block domains linked to alleged copyright infringement. Quad9 said the choice was between crippling penalties and changing its service in ways that would undermine user privacy and security. The case is presented as a warning about the consequences of mandating DNS-level blocking.</span><span class='zh'>瑞士非营利组织 Quad9 表示，将在经历数月诉讼后退出法国免费公共 DNS 解析服务。法国法院因其未屏蔽涉嫌侵犯版权的域名，对 Quad9 处以每日罚款。Quad9 表示，它只能在承受可能令组织陷入困境的处罚，或改变服务并损害用户隐私与安全之间作出选择。该事件被视为强制实施 DNS 层拦截可能带来后果的警示。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://violetpixel.com/blog/2026-11-17-we-need-smart-people-who-give-a-shit/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We need smart people who give a shit</span><span class='zh'>我们需要那些聪明且真正上心的人</span></a><span class="dom">violetpixel.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that complex systems are held together by smart people who care deeply about their work.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，复杂系统依靠聪明且真正关心工作的人维系。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that complex systems are held together by smart people who care deeply about their work. This view becomes especially apparent when examining how difficult systems function, or how close they are to failing. The essay begins from that observation about the people who keep complicated systems operating.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，复杂系统依靠聪明且真正关心工作的人维系。这一点在观察复杂系统如何运转，或如何勉强维持运转时尤其明显。文章以这一关于维持复杂系统运行者的观察为出发点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.inside-db.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Inside Deutsche Bank</span><span class='zh'>德意志银行</span></a><span class="dom">inside-db.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A major investigation into Deutsche Bank is based on a leak of more than 15 million files.</span><span class='zh'>一项针对德意志银行的重大调查建立在超过 1500 万份泄露文件的基础上。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A major investigation into Deutsche Bank is based on a leak of more than 15 million files. The files cover sensitive functions including anti-financial-crime work, sanctions monitoring, internal auditing, and decisions about whether risky clients should remain customers. The investigation portrays flaws in the bank&#x27;s culture, systemic blind spots, and tensions between profit-seeking and compliance.</span><span class='zh'>一项针对德意志银行的重大调查建立在超过 1500 万份泄露文件的基础上。这些文件涉及反金融犯罪、制裁监控、内部审计，以及决定高风险客户是否继续留存等敏感职能。调查呈现出该行文化中的缺陷、系统性盲点，以及追求利润与合规要求之间的矛盾。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://slopstrap.mikelacher.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Slopstrap – Make your site look hastily vibecoded</span><span class='zh'>Slopstrap：让网站看起来像是匆忙用“氛围编程”写出来的</span></a><span class="dom">slopstrap.mikelacher.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Slopstrap describes itself as a CSS framework intended to make websites look as though they were hastily created by an LLM.</span><span class='zh'>Slopstrap 自称是一个 CSS 框架，旨在让网站看起来像是匆忙由 LLM 创建的。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Slopstrap describes itself as a CSS framework intended to make websites look as though they were hastily created by an LLM. It presents the tool as a way to give any project an intentionally AI-generated appearance.</span><span class='zh'>Slopstrap 自称是一个 CSS 框架，旨在让网站看起来像是匆忙由 LLM 创建的。它将自己定位为一种让项目呈现刻意 AI 生成风格的工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nicolasbouliane.com/blog/death-by-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is killing All About Berlin</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在扼杀 All About Berlin</span></a><span class="dom">nicolasbouliane.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The operator of All About Berlin says the site&#x27;s traffic fell by 70% to 75% after years of growth, attributing the decline to Google&#x27;s AI Overviews.</span><span class='zh'>All About Berlin 的运营者表示，该网站在多年增长后，流量因 Google AI Overviews 下跌了 70% 至 75%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The operator of All About Berlin says the site&#x27;s traffic fell by 70% to 75% after years of growth, attributing the decline to Google&#x27;s AI Overviews. Search pages now place an AI-generated answer trained on the site&#x27;s content before the link to the site itself. The change has had a devastating effect on the site&#x27;s traffic.</span><span class='zh'>All About Berlin 的运营者表示，该网站在多年增长后，流量因 Google AI Overviews 下跌了 70% 至 75%。如今搜索页面会先展示基于该网站内容训练的 AI 生成答案，最后才显示指向网站的链接。这一变化对网站流量造成了严重打击。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">31</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://xusheng.dev/posts/windows-coding-agent-telemetry/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Windows 11 is collecting data on which coding agents and developer tools you use</span><span class='zh'>Windows 11 正收集你使用哪些编程代理和开发工具的数据</span></a><span class="dom">xusheng.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering of Windows 11’s MissionControlAggregator found coding-agent and developer-tool usage bits in a diagnostic ETW report.</span><span class='zh'>对 Windows 11 的 MissionControlAggregator 进行逆向分析后，研究者在诊断 ETW 报告中发现了编程代理和开发工具的使用标记。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering of Windows 11’s MissionControlAggregator found coding-agent and developer-tool usage bits in a diagnostic ETW report. The observed list includes Codex, Claude, Copilot, Cursor, Python, .NET, GCC, and Visual Studio Code executables. The finding raises questions about why a Windows system DLL records the use of these tools.</span><span class='zh'>对 Windows 11 的 MissionControlAggregator 进行逆向分析后，研究者在诊断 ETW 报告中发现了编程代理和开发工具的使用标记。相关列表包括 Codex、Claude、Copilot、Cursor，以及 Python、.NET、GCC 和 Visual Studio Code 等可执行文件。这一发现引出了一个问题：Windows 系统 DLL 为何要记录这些工具的使用情况。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>711</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50524736' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>592</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50572355" data-id="50572355" data-tags="Education|Science" data-score="701" data-comments="323" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50572355" data-time="1795167799">
<a class="thumb" href="https://kottke.org/17/06/if-you-cant-explain-something-in-simple-terms-you-dont-understand-it" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='http://kottke.org/plus/misc/images/feynman-blackboard.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='kottke.org' data-c='#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">32</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://kottke.org/17/06/if-you-cant-explain-something-in-simple-terms-you-dont-understand-it" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If you can&#x27;t explain something in simple terms, you don&#x27;t understand it (2017)</span><span class='zh'>如果你无法用简单的话解释某件事，你其实并不理解它（2017）</span></a><span class="dom">kottke.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In the early 1960s, Richard Feynman delivered a series of undergraduate physics lectures.</span><span class='zh'>20 世纪 60 年代初，理查德·费曼为本科生讲授了一系列物理课程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In the early 1960s, Richard Feynman delivered a series of undergraduate physics lectures. Those lectures were later collected into the book The Feynman Lectures on Physics.</span><span class='zh'>20 世纪 60 年代初，理查德·费曼为本科生讲授了一系列物理课程。这些讲座后来被汇编成《费曼物理学讲义》。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>701</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50572355' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>323</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50588982" data-id="50588982" data-tags="Science" data-score="698" data-comments="425" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50588982" data-time="1795267394">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.science.org/content/blog-post/detonating-huge-pile-lead-azide-really" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=science.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>science.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">33</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.science.org/content/blog-post/detonating-huge-pile-lead-azide-really" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Detonating a pile of lead azide</span><span class='zh'>Derek Lowe：引爆一大堆叠氮化铅</span></a><span class="dom">science.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece concerns the detonation of a large pile of lead azide.</span><span class='zh'>这篇内容涉及引爆一大堆叠氮化铅。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece concerns the detonation of a large pile of lead azide. Lead azide is an explosive compound, making the incident a hazardous chemistry event.</span><span class='zh'>这篇内容涉及引爆一大堆叠氮化铅。叠氮化铅是一种爆炸性化合物，因此这是一起危险的化学实验事件。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>698</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50588982' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>425</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50540178" data-id="50540178" data-tags="AI" data-score="690" data-comments="539" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50540178" data-time="1794961078">
<a class="thumb" href="https://revscope.co/blog/search-seo-geo-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=revscope.co&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>revscope.co</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">34</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://revscope.co/blog/search-seo-geo-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s AI answers reducing search traffic by 40%</span><span class='zh'>谷歌 AI 答案让搜索流量下降 40%</span></a><span class="dom">revscope.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A randomized experiment found that Google AI Overviews reduce clicks to external websites by about 40% among US desktop users.</span><span class='zh'>一项随机实验发现，在美国桌面用户中，谷歌 AI Overviews 会让外部网站获得的点击量减少约 40%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A randomized experiment found that Google AI Overviews reduce clicks to external websites by about 40% among US desktop users. The summaries now appear on more than half of the US keywords tracked by DataForSEO, up from 6% two years earlier. Generative engine optimization has not yet demonstrated a way to offset the lost traffic, while AI assistants sent websites only 0.3% of their traffic in March. The findings indicate that traditional search rankings bring substantially fewer visits when an AI answer appears above them.</span><span class='zh'>一项随机实验发现，在美国桌面用户中，谷歌 AI Overviews 会让外部网站获得的点击量减少约 40%。根据 DataForSEO 的跟踪数据，这些摘要如今出现在超过一半的美国关键词结果中，而两年前这一比例仅为 6%。生成式引擎优化尚未证明能弥补这部分流量损失；3 月份，AI 助手为网站带来的流量仅占 0.3%。这表明，当 AI 答案出现在搜索结果上方时，传统排名带来的访问量会显著下降。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>690</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50540178' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>539</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50562113" data-id="50562113" data-tags="Privacy|Legal|Security|Politics" data-score="689" data-comments="361" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50562113" data-time="1795105263">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://macanorak.com/uk-government-forced-to-admit-it-issued-apple-a-secret-encryption-order/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>UK government forced to admit it issued Apple a secret encryption order</span><span class='zh'>英国政府被迫承认曾向苹果发出秘密加密命令</span></a><span class="dom">macanorak.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Investigatory Powers Tribunal rejected the UK government’s attempt to keep its order to Apple secret.</span><span class='zh'>调查权力法庭驳回了英国政府试图对苹果命令保持保密的做法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Investigatory Powers Tribunal rejected the UK government’s attempt to keep its order to Apple secret. The tribunal ruled that the government must acknowledge issuing Apple a Technical Capability Notice under Section 253 of the Investigatory Powers Act 2016. Such notices can require technology companies to alter services to facilitate investigatory powers, in this case amid a dispute over encrypted iCloud data.</span><span class='zh'>调查权力法庭驳回了英国政府试图对苹果命令保持保密的做法。法庭裁定，政府必须承认其依据《2016 年调查权力法》第 253 条向苹果发出过技术能力通知。这类通知可以要求科技公司修改服务，以协助行使调查权；本案涉及加密 iCloud 数据争议。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>689</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50562113' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>361</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50587875" data-id="50587875" data-tags="Hardware" data-score="684" data-comments="316" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50587875" data-time="1795257392">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.tymscar.com/posts/studiodisplayethernet/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>My monitor was cutting my download speed by 75%</span><span class='zh'>我的显示器让下载速度下降了 75%</span></a><span class="dom">blog.tymscar.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 2.5 Gbps symmetrical internet connection produced 2.4 Gbps uploads but only 600 Mbps downloads.</span><span class='zh'>一条上下行对称 2.5 Gbps 的网络连接，上传速度达到 2.4 Gbps，但下载速度只有 600 Mbps。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 2.5 Gbps symmetrical internet connection produced 2.4 Gbps uploads but only 600 Mbps downloads. The same result appeared on another laptop, while the ISP found no problem on its side. The investigation traced the bottleneck to a Studio Display XDR, a small buffer, and a USB burst size of four packets.</span><span class='zh'>一条上下行对称 2.5 Gbps 的网络连接，上传速度达到 2.4 Gbps，但下载速度只有 600 Mbps。在另一台笔记本电脑上测试也得到相同结果，而 ISP 检查后未发现其网络存在问题。调查最终将瓶颈定位到 Studio Display XDR、一个较小的缓冲区，以及每次四个数据包的 USB 突发大小。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>684</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50587875' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>316</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50524767" data-id="50524767" data-tags="Security|Programming|Finance" data-score="669" data-comments="264" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50524767" data-time="1794870034">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.report-uri.com/worlds-largest-brands-infected-with-malware-via-optimizely/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Optimizely compromised, served malware on Zoom, GitLab</span><span class='zh'>Optimizely 遭入侵，Zoom、GitLab 等网站被投放恶意软件</span></a><span class="dom">blog.report-uri.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>On November 16, 2026, malicious JavaScript was served through Optimizely’s Web experimentation platform to visitors of 178 organizations, including Zoom, the BBC, CNN, Salesforce, GitLab, IKEA, HSBC, and others.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年 11 月 16 日，Optimizely 的 Web 实验平台向 178 家机构的网站访客投放了恶意 JavaScript，受影响机构包括 Zoom、BBC、CNN、Salesforce、GitLab、IKEA 和 HSBC 等。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>On November 16, 2026, malicious JavaScript was served through Optimizely’s Web experimentation platform to visitors of 178 organizations, including Zoom, the BBC, CNN, Salesforce, GitLab, IKEA, HSBC, and others. The malware could replace cryptocurrency wallet addresses, skim payment-card data, steal cookies and screenshots, and deliver the Coruna and DarkSword iOS exploit kits on iPhones and iPads. Cryptocurrency had already been stolen in transactions verified as belonging to the attackers.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年 11 月 16 日，Optimizely 的 Web 实验平台向 178 家机构的网站访客投放了恶意 JavaScript，受影响机构包括 Zoom、BBC、CNN、Salesforce、GitLab、IKEA 和 HSBC 等。该恶意软件能够替换加密货币钱包地址、窃取支付卡数据、Cookie 和屏幕截图，并在 iPhone 和 iPad 上投递 Coruna 与 DarkSword iOS 漏洞利用套件。已有加密货币在被确认属于攻击者的交易中遭窃取。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>669</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50524767' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>264</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50572503" data-id="50572503" data-tags="AI|Hardware" data-score="668" data-comments="353" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50572503" data-time="1795168901">
<a class="thumb" href="https://architectlabs.com/blog/frostbite" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://main--gleeful-begonia-6bb5f3.netlify.app/_next/static/media/frostbite-cover.3n9ytugnquhlr.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='architectlabs.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://architectlabs.com/blog/frostbite" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Frostbite: A new CPU architecture discovered, designed and verified by AI</span><span class='zh'>Frostbite：由 AI 发现、设计并验证的新型 CPU 架构</span></a><span class="dom">architectlabs.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Frostbite is a general-purpose CPU built around interaction nets, with its architecture discovered, designed, and verified end to end by AI systems.</span><span class='zh'>Frostbite 是一款基于交互网络的通用 CPU，其架构由 AI 系统端到端完成发现、设计和验证。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Frostbite is a general-purpose CPU built around interaction nets, with its architecture discovered, designed, and verified end to end by AI systems. The project introduces a new instruction set and exploits local steps, explicit dependencies, and single ownership of values. On the reported benchmark, it reached 4.59 times the performance per watt of a modern dual-issue, in-order CPU core.</span><span class='zh'>Frostbite 是一款基于交互网络的通用 CPU，其架构由 AI 系统端到端完成发现、设计和验证。该项目引入了全新的指令集，并利用局部步骤、显式依赖和数值单一所有权等特性。在所报告的基准测试中，它达到了现代双发射顺序执行 CPU 核心 4.59 倍的每瓦性能。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>668</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50572503' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>353</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50532849" data-id="50532849" data-tags="Security" data-score="664" data-comments="748" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532849" data-time="1794929908">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cnn.com/2026/11/17/politics/highly-classified-national-security-communications-network-goes-down" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Highly classified US national security communications network goes down</span><span class='zh'>高度机密的美国国家安全通信网络发生故障</span></a><span class="dom">cnn.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The highly classified network used by the National Security Agency for internal communications is down, according to six people familiar with the matter.</span><span class='zh'>据六名知情人士透露，美国国家安全局用于内部通信的高度机密网络目前发生故障。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The highly classified network used by the National Security Agency for internal communications is down, according to six people familiar with the matter. The reported outage affects a communications system supporting the agency’s internal operations.</span><span class='zh'>据六名知情人士透露，美国国家安全局用于内部通信的高度机密网络目前发生故障。此次中断影响了支持该机构内部运作的通信系统。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://element.io/blog/how-the-european-commission-rolled-out-matrix-to-more-than-40-000-staff/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How the European Commission rolled out Matrix to more than 40k staff</span><span class='zh'>欧洲委员会如何将 Matrix 推广给超过 4 万名员工</span></a><span class="dom">element.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The European Commission moved from a Matrix proof of concept in December 2025 to a sovereign communication tool deployed on every Commission laptop by September 2026.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲委员会从 2025 年 12 月的 Matrix 概念验证起步，到 2026 年 9 月将一套主权通信工具部署到每台委员会笔记本电脑上。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The European Commission moved from a Matrix proof of concept in December 2025 to a sovereign communication tool deployed on every Commission laptop by September 2026. Built on Matrix by Element and operated by the Commission itself, the service now supports more than 40,000 users. Commission officials described the operational challenges of scaling the system and how they addressed them.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲委员会从 2025 年 12 月的 Matrix 概念验证起步，到 2026 年 9 月将一套主权通信工具部署到每台委员会笔记本电脑上。该工具由 Element 基于 Matrix 构建，并由委员会自行运营，目前已服务超过 4 万名用户。委员会官员还介绍了系统扩展过程中遇到的运营难题及应对方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nvidia stopped disclosing its Singapore sales as its revenue reached $104 billion. No further details about the reporting change or its reasons are established here.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达营收达到 1040 亿美元后，停止披露其在新加坡的销售额。现有信息未说明这一报告变化的具体原因或其他细节。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Microsoft Outlook outage meant Phantasialand did not receive notice that excessive chlorine was present in a pool.</span><span class='zh'>微软 Outlook 服务中断，导致 Phantasialand 未能收到泳池氯含量过高的通知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Microsoft Outlook outage meant Phantasialand did not receive notice that excessive chlorine was present in a pool. Fifteen guests were injured as a result.</span><span class='zh'>微软 Outlook 服务中断，导致 Phantasialand 未能收到泳池氯含量过高的通知。结果造成 15 名客人受伤。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50583761" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ask HN: I have been unemployed for 2y. 15 years of experience, US. What to do?</span><span class='zh'>问 HN：我失业两年了，有 15 年经验，在美国该怎么办？</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A senior software engineer in the US DMV area says they have been unemployed for two years after leaving an insurance technology company in January 2025.</span><span class='zh'>一名居住在美国 DMV 地区的资深软件工程师表示，自己在 2025 年 1 月离开一家保险科技公司后已失业两年。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A senior software engineer in the US DMV area says they have been unemployed for two years after leaving an insurance technology company in January 2025. They have 15 years of experience, continue tutoring mathematics, and may soon manage a pizzeria, but have found no software engineering job despite skills in C#/.NET, AWS, and AI. With only four to five months of savings left, they are asking for well-paid alternatives to software engineering or adjacent work.</span><span class='zh'>一名居住在美国 DMV 地区的资深软件工程师表示，自己在 2025 年 1 月离开一家保险科技公司后已失业两年。此人拥有 15 年经验，目前继续辅导数学，并可能很快开始担任披萨店经理，但尽管具备 C#/.NET、AWS 和 AI 技能，仍未找到软件工程工作。由于积蓄只够支撑四到五个月，对方正在寻求软件工程或相关领域之外的高薪替代路径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techstackups.com/articles/quad9-leaves-france-dns-blocking/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DNS resolvers are the wrong place to fight piracy</span><span class='zh'>Quad9 将退出法国：DNS 解析器不该成为打击盗版的场所</span></a><span class="dom">techstackups.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The nonprofit DNS resolver Quad9 will stop answering queries from France on November 24, 2026, rather than comply with a Paris court order sought by beIN Sports.</span><span class='zh'>非营利 DNS 解析器 Quad9 将于 2026 年 11 月 24 日停止响应来自法国的查询，以拒绝执行 beIN Sports 申请的巴黎法院命令。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The nonprofit DNS resolver Quad9 will stop answering queries from France on November 24, 2026, rather than comply with a Paris court order sought by beIN Sports. The court fined Quad9 for refusing to block pirate sports-streaming domains, and roughly 20 similar cases are still pending. The dispute raises concerns that forcing public DNS resolvers to enforce domain blocking could undermine services beyond its intended target.</span><span class='zh'>非营利 DNS 解析器 Quad9 将于 2026 年 11 月 24 日停止响应来自法国的查询，以拒绝执行 beIN Sports 申请的巴黎法院命令。Quad9 因拒绝屏蔽盗版体育流媒体域名而被罚款，法国目前还有约 20 起类似案件待审。这场争议引发了对强制公共 DNS 解析器执行域名屏蔽可能造成更广泛影响的担忧。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>4.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>603</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50545958' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>223</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50513160" data-id="50513160" data-tags="Security|Programming" data-score="598" data-comments="174" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50513160" data-time="1794794688">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://iter.ca/post/ubo-uxss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Universal XSS in uBlock Origin from website-driven element picker</span><span class='zh'>uBlock Origin 的网站驱动元素选择器存在通用 XSS</span></a><span class="dom">iter.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A one-click universal XSS vulnerability affected the Firefox version of uBlock Origin through its element picker.</span><span class='zh'>uBlock Origin 的 Firefox 版本元素选择器存在一个可通过一次点击触发的通用 XSS 漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A one-click universal XSS vulnerability affected the Firefox version of uBlock Origin through its element picker. A malicious website could trick the extension into adding arbitrary trusted filters, which can inject JavaScript into other websites and execute it in their contexts. The issue has been fixed; a related but less severe problem also affected the MV3-based uBO Lite.</span><span class='zh'>uBlock Origin 的 Firefox 版本元素选择器存在一个可通过一次点击触发的通用 XSS 漏洞。恶意网站可以诱使扩展添加任意受信过滤规则，而受信规则能够向其他网站注入 JavaScript，并在这些网站的上下文中执行。该问题现已修复；基于 MV3 的 uBO Lite 也受到一个相关但危害较小的问题影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://manidoraisamy.com/developer-to-architect.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We promoted developers into architects without informing them</span><span class='zh'>我们把开发者提拔成架构师，却没有告诉他们</span></a><span class="dom">manidoraisamy.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that AI-assisted coding shifts developers’ work toward architecture and product judgment.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，AI 辅助编程正在把开发者的工作重心转向架构和产品判断。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that AI-assisted coding shifts developers’ work toward architecture and product judgment. A performance bug in a Google Forms add-on was traced to AI-generated logging that recorded user email information despite Google’s restrictions. The proposed fix is to log only the timestamp, show the welcome screen first, and tell marketing which installation data is unavailable.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，AI 辅助编程正在把开发者的工作重心转向架构和产品判断。一个 Google Forms 插件的性能问题，最终被追溯到 AI 生成的日志代码：它记录了用户邮箱，而 Google 已限制可获取的用户信息。作者提出的修复方案是只记录时间戳、先显示欢迎界面，并告知市场团队安装时无法获取哪些数据。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>583</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50579331' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>310</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50591853" data-id="50591853" data-tags="AI|Programming|Code" data-score="582" data-comments="138" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50591853" data-time="1795284139">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/ShinMegamiBoson/lookatmyquant" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ShinMegamiBoson/lookatmyquant" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Inkling-2-Mini in 5.4 GB Memory</span><span class='zh'>在 5.4 GB 内存中运行 Inkling-2-Mini</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LookAtMyQuant is a quantization compiler that evaluates how much compressing each part of a model affects its output.</span><span class='zh'>LookAtMyQuant 是一个量化编译器，用于评估压缩模型各部分对输出造成的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LookAtMyQuant is a quantization compiler that evaluates how much compressing each part of a model affects its output. It selects codes for individual parts so the model fits a size budget with minimal damage. On Gemma 4 E2B, its GGUF reportedly achieves 12 times lower KL divergence than Q4_K_M at the same file size.</span><span class='zh'>LookAtMyQuant 是一个量化编译器，用于评估压缩模型各部分对输出造成的影响。它会为各个部分选择编码，使模型在满足大小限制的同时尽量减少损失。在 Gemma 4 E2B 上，其 GGUF 文件在相同文件大小下的 KL 散度据称比 Q4_K_M 低 12 倍。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>582</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50591853' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>138</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50594911" data-id="50594911" data-tags="Entertainment" data-score="572" data-comments="198" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50594911" data-time="1795306079">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://signal.org/blog/protecting-your-privacy/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Restricting AI agents in mobile operating systems platforms from capturing data</span><span class='zh'>限制移动操作系统中的 AI 代理捕获数据</span></a><span class="dom">signal.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Signal says it is restricting the latest AI agents built into mobile operating systems from potentially capturing and storing Signal data.</span><span class='zh'>Signal 表示，正在限制移动操作系统中最新的 AI 代理，以防它们捕获并存储 Signal 数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Signal says it is restricting the latest AI agents built into mobile operating systems from potentially capturing and storing Signal data. The move is presented as part of Signal’s broader privacy mission.</span><span class='zh'>Signal 表示，正在限制移动操作系统中最新的 AI 代理，以防它们捕获并存储 Signal 数据。这一举措被视为 Signal 隐私保护使命的一部分。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/claude-black-hole-physics" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://assets.claude.com/57a443b51d13727573b7c1290130d09c5f434b2c.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='anthropic.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">51</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/claude-black-hole-physics" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude solves two open problems in the physics of black holes</span><span class='zh'>Claude 破解黑洞物理学中的两个开放问题</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5.2 is reported to have solved two open problems in theoretical physics involving black holes and the search for quantum gravity.</span><span class='zh'>据称，Claude Fable 5.2 解决了理论物理学中的两个开放问题，涉及黑洞和量子引力理论的探索。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5.2 is reported to have solved two open problems in theoretical physics involving black holes and the search for quantum gravity. The problems sit at the intersection of general relativity and quantum mechanics, whose mathematical incompatibility remains a central challenge in physics. Researchers often use holographic duality and simplified model universes to study these questions.</span><span class='zh'>据称，Claude Fable 5.2 解决了理论物理学中的两个开放问题，涉及黑洞和量子引力理论的探索。这些问题位于广义相对论与量子力学的交叉地带，而两者在数学上的不相容仍是物理学的核心挑战之一。研究人员经常借助全息对偶和简化的模型宇宙来研究这些问题。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://ecohustler.com/article/one-corporation-to-pollute-them-all/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://ecohustler.com/img/asset/bWFpbi9vbmUtY29ycG9yYXRpb24tdG8tcG9sbHV0ZS10aGVtLWFsbC5wbmc/one-corporation-to-pollute-them-all.png?p=og&amp;s=6d596b09b5a7b3028cc8993239fa3d38' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='ecohustler.com' data-c='#10b981' data-tag='Science'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ecohustler.com/article/one-corporation-to-pollute-them-all/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Carnival Cruises emits ten times more sulphur oxide than all of Europe&#x27;s cars (2019)</span><span class='zh'>嘉年华邮轮排放的硫氧化物是欧洲所有汽车总和的十倍（2019）</span></a><span class="dom">ecohustler.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Transport &amp; Environment analysis found that Carnival Corporation emitted nearly 10 times more sulfur oxides around European coasts in 2017 than all 260 million European cars combined.</span><span class='zh'>Transport &amp; Environment 的分析发现，2017 年嘉年华集团在欧洲海岸附近排放的硫氧化物，几乎是欧洲 2.6 亿辆汽车总和的 10 倍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Transport &amp; Environment analysis found that Carnival Corporation emitted nearly 10 times more sulfur oxides around European coasts in 2017 than all 260 million European cars combined. The report attributes the pollution to cruise ships burning dirty bunker fuel. The findings prompted calls for cleaner fuel and stronger controls on shipping emissions.</span><span class='zh'>Transport &amp; Environment 的分析发现，2017 年嘉年华集团在欧洲海岸附近排放的硫氧化物，几乎是欧洲 2.6 亿辆汽车总和的 10 倍。报告将污染归因于邮轮燃烧污染严重的船用燃料。该发现促使人们呼吁使用更清洁的燃料，并加强对航运排放的监管。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>549</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50572116' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>341</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50578358" data-id="50578358" data-tags="Science" data-score="538" data-comments="240" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50578358" data-time="1795195296">
<a class="thumb" href="https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/11/20/unforced-euler-singularity-unforced-navier-stokes-singularity-and-the-generalized-riemann-hypothesis/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=terrytao.wordpress.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>terrytao.wordpress.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/11/20/unforced-euler-singularity-unforced-navier-stokes-singularity-and-the-generalized-riemann-hypothesis/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Unforced Navier-Stokes Singularity and the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis</span><span class='zh'>无外力 Navier–Stokes 奇异性与广义黎曼猜想</span></a><span class="dom">terrytao.wordpress.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Terence Tao lists recent results involving unforced Euler and Navier–Stokes equations, the Riemann Hypothesis, and researchers including Levent Alpöge and Tristan Buckmaster.</span><span class='zh'>Terence Tao 列出了涉及无外力 Euler 方程、Navier–Stokes 方程、黎曼猜想以及 Levent Alpöge、Tristan Buckmaster 等研究者的最新结果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Terence Tao lists recent results involving unforced Euler and Navier–Stokes equations, the Riemann Hypothesis, and researchers including Levent Alpöge and Tristan Buckmaster. He says the results will be reported more widely, while noting that their current level of digestion varies. The post is dated November 20, 2026.</span><span class='zh'>Terence Tao 列出了涉及无外力 Euler 方程、Navier–Stokes 方程、黎曼猜想以及 Levent Alpöge、Tristan Buckmaster 等研究者的最新结果。他表示，这些结果还会在其他渠道广泛报道，同时指出目前对这些成果的理解和整理程度各不相同。该文章发表于 2026 年 11 月 20 日。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Frame is a handmade wooden frame containing a 31.5-inch full-color ePaper display. It is the product’s third release and is now available for pre-order. The first batch is limited to about 470 units, with a few intended to ship by the end of 2026 and most deliveries expected in early 2027.</span><span class='zh'>Frame 是一款采用手工木制外壳的 31.5 英寸全彩电子纸显示器。这是该产品的第三次发布，目前已开放预订。首批约 470 台，计划在 2026 年底前发出少量产品，大多数订单预计于 2027 年初交付。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://macanorak.com/the-macbook-pros-oled-pwm-problem-when-a-better-display-becomes-worse-to-use/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The MacBook Pro&#x27;s OLED PWM problem</span><span class='zh'>MacBook Pro 的 OLED PWM 问题：更好的显示屏为何更难用</span></a><span class="dom">macanorak.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple has introduced tandem OLED technology to the MacBook Pro, alongside the Mini-LED option that remains available.</span><span class='zh'>苹果为 MacBook Pro 引入了串联 OLED 技术，同时继续提供 Mini-LED 版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple has introduced tandem OLED technology to the MacBook Pro, alongside the Mini-LED option that remains available. The display offers individually illuminated pixels, effectively infinite contrast, deeper blacks, higher peak HDR brightness, and touch input, prompting an overwhelmingly positive initial reaction. The source argues that impressive image quality does not necessarily make the screen comfortable for every user, particularly because of its PWM behavior.</span><span class='zh'>苹果为 MacBook Pro 引入了串联 OLED 技术，同时继续提供 Mini-LED 版本。该显示屏具备独立发光像素、近乎无限的对比度、更深的黑色、更高的 HDR 峰值亮度，并首次支持触控，发布初期反响普遍积极。文章指出，出色的画质并不一定意味着所有用户都能舒适使用，尤其是其 PWM 特性可能带来问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/business/2026/nov/20/global-stock-markets-slump-anthropic-ipo-ai-sell-off" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nasdaq erases all 2026 gains as AI sell-off dampens Anthropic’s second day</span><span class='zh'>纳斯达克抹去 2026 年全部涨幅，AI 抛售拖累 Anthropic 上市第二日</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Nasdaq Composite erased all of its gains for 2026 and ended Friday 5% below its value on January 1.</span><span class='zh'>纳斯达克综合指数抹去了 2026 年以来的全部涨幅，周五收盘时较 1 月 1 日的水平低 5%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Nasdaq Composite erased all of its gains for 2026 and ended Friday 5% below its value on January 1. Anthropic&#x27;s stock fell during its second trading day, failing to lift the wider market. The Dow Jones Industrial Average and S&amp;P 500 both closed in correction territory as investors questioned the large sums being committed to AI infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>纳斯达克综合指数抹去了 2026 年以来的全部涨幅，周五收盘时较 1 月 1 日的水平低 5%。Anthropic 股价在上市第二个交易日下跌，未能提振整体市场。道琼斯工业平均指数和标普 500 指数均以进入回调区间收盘，投资者开始质疑投入 AI 基础设施的巨额资金。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">57</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://consumerrights.wiki/w/ID.me_terms_required_for_IRS_sign-in" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ID.me terms required for IRS sign-in</span><span class='zh'>IRS 登录要求接受 ID.me 条款</span></a><span class="dom">consumerrights.wiki</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Taxpayers using ID.me to sign in to IRS online services accept terms that send most disputes to arbitration rather than a judge or jury, unless they opt out within 30 days.</span><span class='zh'>纳税人使用 ID.me 登录美国国税局在线服务时，将接受把大多数争议提交仲裁、而非交由法官或陪审团审理的条款，除非在 30 天内选择退出。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Taxpayers using ID.me to sign in to IRS online services accept terms that send most disputes to arbitration rather than a judge or jury, unless they opt out within 30 days. ID.me&#x27;s privacy policy says it may use information for marketing and transfer information during a sale, merger, or similar transaction. It excludes government identity-verification data from ID.me Shop marketing unless the user consents or another listed exception applies. Login.gov and IRS Direct Pay remain alternatives for IRS access.</span><span class='zh'>纳税人使用 ID.me 登录美国国税局在线服务时，将接受把大多数争议提交仲裁、而非交由法官或陪审团审理的条款，除非在 30 天内选择退出。ID.me 的隐私政策称，其可能将信息用于营销，并在出售、合并或类似交易中转移信息。政府身份验证数据通常不会用于 ID.me Shop 的营销，但用户同意或符合其他列明例外的情况除外。IRS 仍提供 Login.gov 和无需登录的 Direct Pay 作为替代方式。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>492</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50540437' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>259</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50575644" data-id="50575644" data-tags="Web|Politics" data-score="491" data-comments="270" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50575644" data-time="1795187011">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.mozilla.org/en/mozilla/internet-policy/apple-ios-browser-engine-rules/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://blog.mozilla.org/wp-content/blogs.dir/278/files/2026/11/Mozilla_Illustrations_Pixelgram_Arrows_Orange-1082x721.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.mozilla.org' data-c='#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">58</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.mozilla.org/en/mozilla/internet-policy/apple-ios-browser-engine-rules/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why there are still no non-WebKit browsers on iOS</span><span class='zh'>为什么 iOS 上仍然没有非 WebKit 浏览器</span></a><span class="dom">blog.mozilla.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mozilla says that no browser using an engine other than WebKit has launched on iOS, even years after Apple allowed alternative engines in the EU.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 表示，即使苹果在欧盟允许使用替代浏览器引擎多年，iOS 上仍没有采用非 WebKit 引擎的浏览器推出。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mozilla says that no browser using an engine other than WebKit has launched on iOS, even years after Apple allowed alternative engines in the EU. Since the EU’s Digital Markets Act began requiring browser choice in 2024, millions of users have selected Firefox and its daily iOS use has doubled. Apple still requires all iOS browsers to use its own browser engine.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 表示，即使苹果在欧盟允许使用替代浏览器引擎多年，iOS 上仍没有采用非 WebKit 引擎的浏览器推出。自欧盟《数字市场法》于 2024 年开始要求提供浏览器选择以来，已有数百万用户选择 Firefox，其在 iOS 上的日活跃使用量也翻了一倍。苹果仍要求所有 iOS 浏览器使用自家的浏览器引擎。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>491</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50575644' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>270</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50601525" data-id="50601525" data-tags="AI" data-score="485" data-comments="334" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50601525" data-time="1795367187">
<a class="thumb" href="https://openai.com/index/perplexity-and-intelligence/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/6rIIhCRSKrhIfe385Onmcz/aca2d190553c62fd5d49efb5d5ce1eb6/perplexity-and-intelligence-social.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='openai.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">59</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/perplexity-and-intelligence/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Perplexity and Intelligence</span><span class='zh'>困惑度与智能</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI chief scientist Jakub Pachocki describes modern AI as largely based on training a compressor or predictor over a very large corpus of data.</span><span class='zh'>文章探讨困惑度如何衡量 AI 模型的进步，以及它如何帮助开发者建立评估新兴能力的共同方法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI chief scientist Jakub Pachocki describes modern AI as largely based on training a compressor or predictor over a very large corpus of data.</span><span class='zh'>文章探讨困惑度如何衡量 AI 模型的进步，以及它如何帮助开发者建立评估新兴能力的共同方法。OpenAI 首席科学家 Jakub Pachocki 将现代 AI 描述为主要通过在超大规模数据语料上训练压缩器或预测器来构建。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://europeanbusinessmagazine.com/ai-agents-better-questions-kpmg/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI agents ask better questions than KPMG accountants in audit trial</span><span class='zh'>审计试验中，AI 智能体比 KPMG 会计师提出了更好的问题</span></a><span class="dom">europeanbusinessmagazine.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>KPMG UK tested more than 100 AI agents on the accounts of a major UK-listed company.</span><span class='zh'>KPMG 英国分部让 100 多个 AI 智能体审查一家英国上市大型公司的账目。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>KPMG UK tested more than 100 AI agents on the accounts of a major UK-listed company. The agents sometimes investigated financial issues more deeply and asked better questions than the human audit team. The experiment raises questions about how automation could change the economics of auditing as machines take on more analytical work.</span><span class='zh'>KPMG 英国分部让 100 多个 AI 智能体审查一家英国上市大型公司的账目。在试验中，智能体有时比人类审计团队更深入地调查财务问题，并提出更好的问题。随着机器承担更多分析工作，这项试验引发了人们对自动化将如何改变审计行业经济模式的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ounapuu.ee/posts/2026/11/19/unsafeguards/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fuck Your AI Safeguards</span><span class='zh'>去你的 AI 安全护栏</span></a><span class="dom">ounapuu.ee</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A developer describes asking Claude Code to identify easy security improvements in a project and finding a one-line fix.</span><span class='zh'>一名开发者让 Claude Code 找出项目中容易修复的安全问题，并发现了一个只需一行代码即可解决的问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer describes asking Claude Code to identify easy security improvements in a project and finding a one-line fix. The attempt was then blocked by AI safeguards, despite the developer saying they controlled the product and were trying to address security and compliance issues.</span><span class='zh'>一名开发者让 Claude Code 找出项目中容易修复的安全问题，并发现了一个只需一行代码即可解决的问题。随后，这一操作被 AI 安全护栏阻止，尽管开发者表示自己控制着该产品，目的是修复安全问题并满足合规要求。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21885" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=arxiv.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>arxiv.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21885" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Language Models Can Rewrite Their Own Weights</span><span class='zh'>语言模型可以重写自己的权重</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers describe a self-rewriting method in which a language model diagnoses its failures and edits its own weights without gradients or another training run.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员介绍了一种自我重写方法：语言模型无需梯度或重新训练，就能诊断自身失败并修改权重。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers describe a self-rewriting method in which a language model diagnoses its failures and edits its own weights without gradients or another training run. An open 27-billion-parameter model improved its accuracy by 6.6 points on unseen problems after its own edit. Across eight sessions, it repeatedly tested edits and retained only those that improved performance, and it restored a damaged copy to more than 80% of its original image-task accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员介绍了一种自我重写方法：语言模型无需梯度或重新训练，就能诊断自身失败并修改权重。一个拥有 270 亿参数的开放模型在自我修改后，在未见过的问题上的准确率提高了 6.6 个百分点。模型在八个连续会话中反复测试修改，只保留能提升性能的版本，并将一个受损副本恢复到原始图像任务准确率的 80% 以上。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://steveblank.com/2026/11/15/whats-next-for-nvidia/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What&#x27;s Next for Nvidia?</span><span class='zh'>英伟达的下一步是什么？</span></a><span class="dom">steveblank.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nvidia’s board announced that co-founder Jensen Huang had resigned after 33 years at the company.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达董事会宣布，联合创始人黄仁勋在公司任职 33 年后辞职。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nvidia’s board announced that co-founder Jensen Huang had resigned after 33 years at the company. The change raises questions about what happens when a company loses a visionary chief executive. The supplied discussion argues that Nvidia could risk losing innovation and relying on momentum and its brand.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达董事会宣布，联合创始人黄仁勋在公司任职 33 年后辞职。这一变动引发了人们对公司失去具有远见的首席执行官后将如何发展的疑问。相关讨论认为，英伟达可能面临创新减弱、转而依赖既有势头和品牌的风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://phrack.org/issues/73/introduction_md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Phrack 73</span><span class='zh'>Phrack 73</span></a><span class="dom">phrack.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Phrack&#x27;s latest release follows its 40th-anniversary relaunch, which included a printed edition, conference events, talks, and a capture-the-flag challenge.</span><span class='zh'>Phrack 的最新一期延续了其 40 周年重启活动，该活动包括纸质版、会议活动、演讲和夺旗挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Phrack&#x27;s latest release follows its 40th-anniversary relaunch, which included a printed edition, conference events, talks, and a capture-the-flag challenge. The relaunch aimed to put new research in readers&#x27; hands and encourage people to read, research, write, and contribute again. Its stated goal is to inspire readers to take systems apart and investigate how they work.</span><span class='zh'>Phrack 的最新一期延续了其 40 周年重启活动，该活动包括纸质版、会议活动、演讲和夺旗挑战。重启的目标是把新的研究成果交到读者手中，并鼓励人们重新阅读、研究、写作和贡献。其明确目标是激发读者拆解系统、探究其工作方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ductus.sorenblank.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Handwriting, as a Font</span><span class='zh'>把手写字迹变成字体</span></a><span class="dom">ductus.sorenblank.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This browser tool turns a user’s handwriting into a font.</span><span class='zh'>这个浏览器工具可以将用户的手写字迹转换为字体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This browser tool turns a user’s handwriting into a font. Users write each letter in the browser and download the result as OTF or WOFF2. No account or upload is required, and the letters remain on the device.</span><span class='zh'>这个浏览器工具可以将用户的手写字迹转换为字体。用户在浏览器中书写每个字母，然后下载 OTF 或 WOFF2 格式的字体。它无需账户或上传，字母会保留在设备上。</span></p>
