<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bruceroettgers.eu/blog/protomaps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Protomaps: Self-hosting map tiles has gotten cheap and easy</span><span class='zh'>Protomaps：自托管地图瓦片如今既便宜又简单</span></a><span class="dom">bruceroettgers.eu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Protomaps uses PMTiles, a tile format that stores a map in a single file and serves it through HTTP Range requests.</span><span class='zh'>Protomaps 使用 PMTiles，这种瓦片格式将地图存储在单个文件中，并通过 HTTP Range 请求提供服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Protomaps uses PMTiles, a tile format that stores a map in a single file and serves it through HTTP Range requests. The file can be uploaded to a storage bucket and cached by compatible CDNs, while an edge function can translate conventional x/y/z tile URLs for services such as Cloudflare. This makes map data portable and inexpensive to deploy compared with usage-priced commercial APIs.</span><span class='zh'>Protomaps 使用 PMTiles，这种瓦片格式将地图存储在单个文件中，并通过 HTTP Range 请求提供服务。该文件可以上传到存储桶并由支持相关缓存的 CDN 缓存；对于 Cloudflare 等服务，也可以用边缘函数将传统的 x/y/z 瓦片 URL 转换为 Range 请求。与按使用量收费的商业 API 相比，这让地图数据更易移植，也更便宜易部署。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>455</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50509837' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>83</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50539610" data-id="50539610" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="450" data-comments="98" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50539610" data-time="1794957529">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theregister.com/saas/2026/11/16/microsoft-blames-monster-email-outage-on-software-defect-introduced-in-july-fix/5310026" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft blames monster email outage on software defect introduced in July fix</span><span class='zh'>微软称大规模电子邮件故障源于 7 月修复引入的软件缺陷</span></a><span class="dom">theregister.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft blamed last week’s major Microsoft 365 email outage on a defect introduced by a July 2026 change.</span><span class='zh'>微软将上周 Microsoft 365 大规模电子邮件故障归因于 2026 年 7 月一次变更引入的软件缺陷。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft blamed last week’s major Microsoft 365 email outage on a defect introduced by a July 2026 change. The defect remained dormant in network gateway servers until increased load and reduced capacity triggered it, causing a day-long disruption. Microsoft’s preliminary post-incident report said the change had passed load testing and initially caused no impact after deployment.</span><span class='zh'>微软将上周 Microsoft 365 大规模电子邮件故障归因于 2026 年 7 月一次变更引入的软件缺陷。该缺陷一直潜伏在网络网关服务器中，直到负载上升、容量下降时被触发，造成持续一天的中断。微软的初步事后报告称，该变更通过了负载测试，部署后最初也没有造成影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://burntsushi.net/jiff/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jiff 1.0: A datetime library for Rust</span><span class='zh'>Jiff 1.0：Rust 的日期时间库</span></a><span class="dom">burntsushi.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jiff 1.0 is a datetime library for Rust that puts time-zone handling at the center of its API.</span><span class='zh'>Jiff 1.0 是一个 Rust 日期时间库，其 API 将时区处理置于核心位置。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jiff 1.0 is a datetime library for Rust that puts time-zone handling at the center of its API. It automatically addresses many common daylight-saving-time problems and is already widely adopted in the Rust ecosystem. The name is pronounced like “gif,” with a soft “g,” as in “gem.”</span><span class='zh'>Jiff 1.0 是一个 Rust 日期时间库，其 API 将时区处理置于核心位置。它会自动处理许多与夏令时有关的常见问题，并已在 Rust 生态中得到广泛采用。Jiff 的发音类似带软 g 音的“gif”，就像“gem”中的 g。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.oreilly.com/radar/what-happened-to-silicon-valley/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What Happened to Silicon Valley?</span><span class='zh'>硅谷发生了什么？</span></a><span class="dom">oreilly.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The lecture traces Silicon Valley&#x27;s shift from customer-centered innovation toward extraction driven by shareholder capitalism, monopoly ambitions, and hype.</span><span class='zh'>这场演讲追溯了硅谷如何从以客户为中心的创新，转向由股东资本主义、垄断野心和炒作驱动的价值攫取。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The lecture traces Silicon Valley&#x27;s shift from customer-centered innovation toward extraction driven by shareholder capitalism, monopoly ambitions, and hype. It connects that evolution to the current AI boom and argues that technology&#x27;s value depends on surrounding rules, incentives, and interfaces. Its proposed direction emphasizes openness, portability, interoperability, and a circulatory economy of value.</span><span class='zh'>这场演讲追溯了硅谷如何从以客户为中心的创新，转向由股东资本主义、垄断野心和炒作驱动的价值攫取。演讲将这一变化与当前 AI 热潮联系起来，并认为技术价值取决于周围的规则、激励机制和界面。其主张的方向强调开放性、可移植性、互操作性，以及价值循环流动的经济模式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/anthropic-prices-initial-public-offering" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic prices initial public offering</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 确定 IPO 发行价</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic priced its initial public offering at $83 per Class A share.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 将其 A 类普通股 IPO 发行价定为每股 83 美元。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic priced its initial public offering at $83 per Class A share. The offering consists of 980 million shares, including 956,351,354 sold by Anthropic and 23,648,646 sold by existing stockholders. Trading is expected to begin on Nasdaq under the symbol “ANT” on November 19, 2026, with closing expected the following day and an underwriter option for up to 147 million additional shares.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 将其 A 类普通股 IPO 发行价定为每股 83 美元。本次发行共 9.8 亿股，其中 Anthropic 出售 956,351,354 股，部分现有股东出售 23,648,646 股。股票预计于 2026 年 11 月 19 日以“ANT”为代码在纳斯达克开始交易，发行预计于次日完成，承销商另拥有最多追加购买 1.47 亿股的 30 天期权。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sciencenorway.no/culture-history-history-of-science/350-years-ago-this-man-discovered-that-light-has-a-finite-speed/2737969" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>350 years ago, Ole Rømer discovered that light has a finite speed</span><span class='zh'>350 年前，奥勒·罗默发现光速有限</span></a><span class="dom">sciencenorway.no</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In September 1676, Ole Rømer predicted that Jupiter&#x27;s moon Io would emerge from the planet&#x27;s shadow 10 minutes later than expected.</span><span class='zh'>1676 年 9 月，奥勒·罗默预测木星卫星木卫一从行星阴影中出现的时间会比预期晚 10 分钟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In September 1676, Ole Rømer predicted that Jupiter&#x27;s moon Io would emerge from the planet&#x27;s shadow 10 minutes later than expected. He presented the prediction to members of the Académie des Sciences in Paris. The observation demonstrated that light takes time to travel.</span><span class='zh'>1676 年 9 月，奥勒·罗默预测木星卫星木卫一从行星阴影中出现的时间会比预期晚 10 分钟。他将这一预测提交给巴黎科学院成员。这一观测证明了光的传播需要时间。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://em-online.bearblog.dev/i-can-tell-you-wrote-this-yourself/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>&quot;I can tell you wrote this yourself&quot;</span><span class='zh'>“我看得出来这是你亲自写的”</span></a><span class="dom">em-online.bearblog.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A supervisor told the author, “I can tell you wrote this yourself,” while reviewing a progress report.</span><span class='zh'>导师在审阅作者的进度报告时说：“我看得出来这是你亲自写的。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A supervisor told the author, “I can tell you wrote this yourself,” while reviewing a progress report. The roughly 10-page report concerned the control of flapping-wing micro aerial vehicles. The author had written about 5,000 words in 48 stressful hours after mistakenly setting the deadline a week early.</span><span class='zh'>导师在审阅作者的进度报告时说：“我看得出来这是你亲自写的。”这份约 10 页的报告主题是扑翼微型飞行器的控制。作者误把截止日期设早了一周，随后在紧张的 48 小时内写下了约 5000 字。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://notnottalmud.substack.com/p/getting-the-mind-to-steer-the-body" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Getting the mind to steer the body to steer you</span><span class='zh'>让心智操控身体，再让身体引导你</span></a><span class="dom">notnottalmud.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The discussion argues that people’s bodies often steer their behavior in daily life.</span><span class='zh'>这段讨论认为，人们的身体在日常生活中往往会反过来引导自己的行为。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The discussion argues that people’s bodies often steer their behavior in daily life. It connects this idea with earlier writing about manifesting, personal agency, and gaining the benefits of wearables without owning one. The participants describe mindset as a central thread linking these topics.</span><span class='zh'>这段讨论认为，人们的身体在日常生活中往往会反过来引导自己的行为。它将这一想法与此前关于显化、个人能动性以及不购买设备也能获得可穿戴设备益处的文章联系起来。参与者认为，心态是串联这些主题的核心线索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://verialabs.com/ruby2shell/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ruby2ßhell: Pre-authentication RCE on stock Rails</span><span class='zh'>Ruby2ßhell：原生 Rails 上的预认证远程代码执行</span></a><span class="dom">verialabs.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ruby2Shell is a one-byte heap overflow in Ruby&#x27;s string case-conversion code.</span><span class='zh'>Ruby2Shell 是 Ruby 字符串大小写转换代码中的单字节堆溢出漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ruby2Shell is a one-byte heap overflow in Ruby&#x27;s string case-conversion code. In common Ruby web frameworks and self-hosted software, the flaw can be reached without authentication when affected configurations or application code process strings in a legacy encoding. The advisory lists popular software whose latest versions bundle an affected Ruby.</span><span class='zh'>Ruby2Shell 是 Ruby 字符串大小写转换代码中的单字节堆溢出漏洞。在常见 Ruby Web 框架和自托管软件中，受影响的默认配置或处理旧式编码字符串的应用代码可能让攻击者无需认证触发该漏洞。公告列出了最新版本仍捆绑受影响 Ruby 的热门软件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://kapteko.com/15-million-webservers/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>15M web servers on one VM – smoltcp, DPDK, AF_XDP</span><span class='zh'>一台虚拟机运行 1500 万个 Web 服务器——smoltcp、DPDK 与 AF_XDP</span></a><span class="dom">kapteko.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project aims to run 15 million web servers on one virtual machine with 10 GB of RAM and a single CPU core.</span><span class='zh'>该项目的目标是在一台配备 10 GB 内存和单个 CPU 核心的虚拟机上运行 1500 万个 Web 服务器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project aims to run 15 million web servers on one virtual machine with 10 GB of RAM and a single CPU core. It explores kernel bypass and moving the TCP/IP stack into user space, using technologies such as smoltcp, DPDK, and AF_XDP. The stated goal is to understand how far server density can be pushed, rather than to deploy a conventional web service.</span><span class='zh'>该项目的目标是在一台配备 10 GB 内存和单个 CPU 核心的虚拟机上运行 1500 万个 Web 服务器。项目探索内核旁路以及将 TCP/IP 协议栈移到用户态，并使用 smoltcp、DPDK 和 AF_XDP 等技术。其目标是研究服务器密度的极限，而不是部署传统的 Web 服务。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.postgresql.org/docs/release/19.0/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PostgreSQL 19</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL 19</span></a><span class="dom">postgresql.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PostgreSQL 19 introduces a new REPACK command that reclaims disk space and reorganizes table contents.</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL 19 引入了新的 REPACK 命令，用于回收磁盘空间并重新组织表内容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PostgreSQL 19 introduces a new REPACK command that reclaims disk space and reorganizes table contents. REPACK can improve query performance, and REPACK (CONCURRENTLY) allows this work without blocking reads and writes to the table.</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL 19 引入了新的 REPACK 命令，用于回收磁盘空间并重新组织表内容。REPACK 可以改善查询性能，而 REPACK（CONCURRENTLY）允许在不阻塞表读写的情况下完成这项工作。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ocw.mit.edu/courses/res-16-002-how-to-cad-almost-anything-january-iap-2024/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How to CAD Almost Anything</span><span class='zh'>如何为几乎任何东西创建 CAD 模型</span></a><span class="dom">ocw.mit.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This workshop introduces computer-aided design through reverse-engineering projects involving objects such as mugs, bottles, rockets, jet-engine blades, test dummies, and animated scenes.</span><span class='zh'>这门工作坊通过对杯子、瓶子、火箭、喷气发动机叶片、测试假人和动画场景等物体进行逆向工程，介绍计算机辅助设计。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This workshop introduces computer-aided design through reverse-engineering projects involving objects such as mugs, bottles, rockets, jet-engine blades, test dummies, and animated scenes. It emphasizes planning, independent thinking, and choosing appropriate tools rather than simply following engineering drawings. The material also includes interactive examples and links to SolidWorks and Creo resources.</span><span class='zh'>这门工作坊通过对杯子、瓶子、火箭、喷气发动机叶片、测试假人和动画场景等物体进行逆向工程，介绍计算机辅助设计。课程强调规划、独立思考和选择合适工具，而不是机械地遵循工程图纸。材料还包含互动示例，以及 SolidWorks 和 Creo 资源链接。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Burger King and McDonald’s were not hacked.</span><span class='zh'>汉堡王和麦当劳并未遭到黑客入侵。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Burger King and McDonald’s were not hacked. Instead, someone re-registered Bitly links posted in their 2011 tweets. The same person or group has replaced 57 of 97 links in old tweets by Donald Trump.</span><span class='zh'>汉堡王和麦当劳并未遭到黑客入侵。有人重新注册了它们在 2011 年推文中使用的 Bitly 链接。同样的问题还影响了唐纳德·特朗普旧推文中的 97 个链接，其中 57 个已被替换。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openjdk/jdk/pull/32781" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Someone reported java4shell bug in September 2026</span><span class='zh'>有人在 2026 年 9 月报告了 java4shell 漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An OpenJDK pull request reports a heap-corruption bug in the x86-64 AVX-512 VBMI Base64 decoder.</span><span class='zh'>一份 OpenJDK 拉取请求报告了 x86-64 AVX-512 VBMI Base64 解码器中的堆损坏漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An OpenJDK pull request reports a heap-corruption bug in the x86-64 AVX-512 VBMI Base64 decoder. The flaw appears when invalid input leaves a short padded tail, causing the decoder to calculate a negative output size and write beyond the logical output buffer before returning to Java. Valid inputs with lengths that are multiples of four do not trigger the issue.</span><span class='zh'>一份 OpenJDK 拉取请求报告了 x86-64 AVX-512 VBMI Base64 解码器中的堆损坏漏洞。当无效输入留下很短的填充尾部时，解码器可能计算出负的输出大小，并在返回 Java 之前写出逻辑输出缓冲区边界。长度为 4 的倍数的有效输入不会触发该问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>372</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50558906' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>95</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50536387" data-id="50536387" data-tags="Programming|Web" data-score="371" data-comments="116" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50536387" data-time="1794941777">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.solidjs.com/blog/solid-2-0-the-long-hold/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Solid 2.0</span><span class='zh'>Solid 2.0</span></a><span class="dom">solidjs.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Solid 2.0 has reached a stable release after five years of development.</span><span class='zh'>Solid 2.0 在经过五年开发后达到稳定版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Solid 2.0 has reached a stable release after five years of development. It adds asynchronous work in the reactive graph, server functions, agent-oriented diagnostics, and a smaller API. Solid&#x27;s original fine-grained, signal-based approach has since become more common across frontend frameworks. The release builds on that model while simplifying the framework&#x27;s interface.</span><span class='zh'>Solid 2.0 在经过五年开发后达到稳定版本。它加入了响应式图中的异步能力、服务器函数、面向代理的诊断工具，并缩小了 API。Solid 最初采用的细粒度信号式方案如今已在多个前端框架中变得普遍。此次发布在延续这一模型的同时简化了框架接口。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">85</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openssl/openssl/pull/33652" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenSSL: Buffer overflow in EVP_EncodeBlock() (CVE-2026-121481, CVSS 9.8)</span><span class='zh'>OpenSSL：EVP_EncodeBlock() 中的缓冲区溢出（CVE-2026-121481，CVSS 9.8）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A proposed OpenSSL fix addresses a buffer overflow in EVP_EncodeBlock(), tracked as CVE-2026-121481 with a CVSS score of 9.8.</span><span class='zh'>一项拟议的 OpenSSL 修复针对 EVP_EncodeBlock() 中的缓冲区溢出漏洞，该漏洞编号为 CVE-2026-121481，CVSS 评分为 9.8。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A proposed OpenSSL fix addresses a buffer overflow in EVP_EncodeBlock(), tracked as CVE-2026-121481 with a CVSS score of 9.8. The change makes the function return -1 for invalid inputs, including null buffers, negative lengths, and encoded sizes that do not fit in an int. The security advisory was still pending when the fix was proposed.</span><span class='zh'>一项拟议的 OpenSSL 修复针对 EVP_EncodeBlock() 中的缓冲区溢出漏洞，该漏洞编号为 CVE-2026-121481，CVSS 评分为 9.8。修复会让函数在输入无效时返回 -1，包括缓冲区为空、长度为负数或编码后大小无法放入 int 的情况。提出修复时，安全公告仍在等待发布。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ninlive.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>NINLIVE: The Nine Inch Nails live archive</span><span class='zh'>NINLIVE：九寸钉现场档案</span></a><span class="dom">ninlive.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NINLIVE is a fan-run archive of Nine Inch Nails concerts, setlists, recordings, posters, and photos from 1988 onward.</span><span class='zh'>NINLIVE 是一个由粉丝运营的九寸钉现场档案，收录自 1988 年以来的演唱会、歌单、录音、海报和照片。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NINLIVE is a fan-run archive of Nine Inch Nails concerts, setlists, recordings, posters, and photos from 1988 onward. It now lets users stream live recordings from 1988 through 2026, in addition to downloading them. Users can log in with Discord to save and organize favorites.</span><span class='zh'>NINLIVE 是一个由粉丝运营的九寸钉现场档案，收录自 1988 年以来的演唱会、歌单、录音、海报和照片。该站现在除了提供下载，还支持在线播放 1988 至 2026 年的现场录音。用户可以使用 Discord 登录，以保存和整理喜欢的录音。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.yahoo.com/news/world/articles/russia-reportedly-planned-mass-casualty-090500574.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Russia reportedly planned mass-casualty attack in Poland to be blamed on Ukraine</span><span class='zh'>据报道，俄罗斯曾策划在波兰制造大规模伤亡并嫁祸乌克兰</span></a><span class="dom">yahoo.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Russian intelligence services reportedly planned a sabotage operation on Polish territory near the border with Russia’s Kaliningrad Oblast.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，俄罗斯情报部门曾计划在波兰境内、靠近俄罗斯加里宁格勒州边境的地区实施破坏行动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Russian intelligence services reportedly planned a sabotage operation on Polish territory near the border with Russia’s Kaliningrad Oblast. The operation was reportedly intended to cause mass casualties in late October or early November and be blamed on Ukraine. The report comes from the Polish newspaper Rzeczpospolita, as cited by European Pravda.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，俄罗斯情报部门曾计划在波兰境内、靠近俄罗斯加里宁格勒州边境的地区实施破坏行动。据称，该行动计划于 10 月下旬或 11 月上旬制造大规模伤亡，并将责任嫁祸给乌克兰。相关消息来自波兰报纸《共和国报》，由 European Pravda 引述。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scottaaronson.blog/?p=10344" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The stochastic parrots have apparently proved the Riemann Hypothesis</span><span class='zh'>随机鹦鹉似乎证明了黎曼猜想</span></a><span class="dom">scottaaronson.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post uses the phrase “stochastic parrots” while discussing the apparent claim that the Riemann Hypothesis has been proved.</span><span class='zh'>文章用“随机鹦鹉”这一说法讨论黎曼猜想似乎已被证明的说法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post uses the phrase “stochastic parrots” while discussing the apparent claim that the Riemann Hypothesis has been proved. It quotes Bernhard Riemann’s 1859 remark that he had set aside the search for a rigorous proof. An update notes that many people on social media interpreted the phrase literally rather than as irony or sarcasm.</span><span class='zh'>文章用“随机鹦鹉”这一说法讨论黎曼猜想似乎已被证明的说法。文中引用了 Bernhard Riemann 1859 年的言论：他曾暂时放下寻找严格证明的工作。作者在更新中指出，许多社交媒体用户将这一说法按字面理解，而没有看出其中的反讽或讽刺意味。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>345</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50581084' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>335</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50532086" data-id="50532086" data-tags="Open Source|AI|Programming|DevOps|Energy" data-score="344" data-comments="234" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532086" data-time="1794926736">
<a class="thumb" href="https://jordankrueger.com/blog/better-open-source-not-more-apps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://jordankrueger.com/images/gallery/blog-better-open-source-not-more-apps.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='jordankrueger.com' data-c='#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">91</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jordankrueger.com/blog/better-open-source-not-more-apps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We don&#x27;t need more apps, we need better open source</span><span class='zh'>我们不需要更多应用，而需要更好的开源软件</span></a><span class="dom">jordankrueger.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI has made it easy to build small applications over a weekend, such as habit trackers, to-do lists, and note-taking tools.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能让人们可以在一个周末轻松构建习惯追踪器、待办事项和笔记应用等小型软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI has made it easy to build small applications over a weekend, such as habit trackers, to-do lists, and note-taking tools. The author argues that more of this energy should go toward improving the open-source software people already rely on, making it faster, lighter, and easier to run. The point is not to discourage people from building, but to redirect some of that effort toward shared infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能让人们可以在一个周末轻松构建习惯追踪器、待办事项和笔记应用等小型软件。作者认为，更多这类精力应投入到改进人们已经依赖的开源软件上，让它们更快、更轻量、更易运行。重点不是阻止人们开发新东西，而是将部分努力转向共享基础设施。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Energy' style='--c:#eab308'>Energy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>344</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532086' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>234</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50520866" data-id="50520866" data-tags="Programming|DevOps" data-score="340" data-comments="218" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50520866" data-time="1794853037">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.breck-mckye.com/blog/2026/11/software-is-breaking-down-at-a-systemic-level/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.breck-mckye.com/garland.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='breck-mckye.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">92</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.breck-mckye.com/blog/2026/11/software-is-breaking-down-at-a-systemic-level/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Software is breaking down at a systemic level</span><span class='zh'>软件正在系统性崩坏</span></a><span class="dom">breck-mckye.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that recent failures point to software problems at a systemic level.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，近期的多起故障表明软件正面临系统性问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that recent failures point to software problems at a systemic level. The examples cited are a two-day global Microsoft outage and an FAA ground stop that halted all flights in the United States. These events are presented as a recent combination of major technology and infrastructure disruptions.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，近期的多起故障表明软件正面临系统性问题。文章举出的例子包括持续两天的全球微软服务中断，以及导致美国所有航班停飞的 FAA 地面停飞令。这些事件被视为近期重大技术和基础设施中断的集中体现。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>5.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>340</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50520866' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>218</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50532191" data-id="50532191" data-tags="AI" data-score="337" data-comments="118" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532191" data-time="1794927223">
<a class="thumb" href="https://runta.com/blog/introducing-frontierharness-eval-v1-1/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://runta.com/social/blog-introducing-frontierharness-eval-v1-1.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='runta.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">93</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://runta.com/blog/introducing-frontierharness-eval-v1-1/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What is the best harness for each model?</span><span class='zh'>每种模型最适合什么 harness？</span></a><span class="dom">runta.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>FrontierHarness Eval v1.1 tested 22 coding-agent harnesses with five models across the same 62 tasks, repeating each test four times for 27,280 runs.</span><span class='zh'>FrontierHarness Eval v1.1 使用 5 个模型，在相同的 62 项任务上测试了 22 个编码代理 harness，每项测试重复 4 次，共运行 27,280 次。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>FrontierHarness Eval v1.1 tested 22 coding-agent harnesses with five models across the same 62 tasks, repeating each test four times for 27,280 runs. The best-value harness varied by model, and cost rankings also shifted substantially between models. With the model held constant, the most expensive harness cost 1.8 to 5 times more per task than the cheapest. The results suggest that changing models may also require reconsidering the harness.</span><span class='zh'>FrontierHarness Eval v1.1 使用 5 个模型，在相同的 62 项任务上测试了 22 个编码代理 harness，每项测试重复 4 次，共运行 27,280 次。性价比最高的 harness 因模型而异，成本排名也会随模型大幅变化。在固定模型的情况下，最贵 harness 的单任务成本是最便宜者的 1.8 至 5 倍。结果表明，更换模型时可能也需要重新评估所用的 harness。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>337</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532191' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>118</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50522643" data-id="50522643" data-tags="Science" data-score="337" data-comments="138" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50522643" data-time="1794860538">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ruby-lang.org/en/news/2026/11/18/multiple-vulnerabilities-in-regexp-and-string/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Multiple vulnerabilities in Regexp and String case conversion</span><span class='zh'>Regexp 和字符串大小写转换中的多个漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">ruby-lang.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Seven vulnerabilities have been found in Ruby’s regular expression engine and string case-conversion functionality.</span><span class='zh'>Ruby 的正则表达式引擎和字符串大小写转换功能中发现了七个漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Seven vulnerabilities have been found in Ruby’s regular expression engine and string case-conversion functionality. Proofs of concept show that some of them can trigger remote code execution. Ruby released versions 4.0.10, 3.4.13, and 3.3.14 to fix the issues and recommends upgrading; CVE identifiers have not yet been assigned.</span><span class='zh'>Ruby 的正则表达式引擎和字符串大小写转换功能中发现了七个漏洞。概念验证表明，其中一些漏洞可用于触发远程代码执行。Ruby 已发布 4.0.10、3.4.13 和 3.3.14 修复这些问题，并建议用户升级；这些漏洞尚未分配 CVE 编号。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://globalcommercegraph.com/writing/the-ninety-eight-percent-slot-machine" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The 98% Slot Machine: How Paid Advertising Became an Extraction Tax</span><span class='zh'>98% 的老虎机：付费广告如何变成一种抽取税</span></a><span class="dom">globalcommercegraph.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The essay argues that advertising auctions are calibrated to pay out just enough to keep advertisers participating.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为，广告竞价机制被调校到只支付足以让广告主继续参与的回报。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The essay argues that advertising auctions are calibrated to pay out just enough to keep advertisers participating. It says auction winners do not necessarily earn profits, but instead gain the opportunity to act as unpaid fulfillment contractors for Google and Meta.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为，广告竞价机制被调校到只支付足以让广告主继续参与的回报。它指出，竞价获胜者未必能获得利润，反而可能只是获得了为 Google 和 Meta 充当无偿履约承包商的机会。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://curiousbits.nl/blog/do-not-buy-ram-compress/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Do not buy RAM, compress</span><span class='zh'>别买内存，压缩它</span></a><span class="dom">curiousbits.nl</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author turned to zram after running out of memory while running increasingly parallel AI workloads.</span><span class='zh'>在运行并行度越来越高的 AI 工作负载时遇到内存不足后，作者转而使用 zram。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author turned to zram after running out of memory while running increasingly parallel AI workloads. Zram compresses memory and is already used by Windows, macOS, and many Android devices, but it is not enabled by default in many Linux distributions. Fedora and Pop!_OS enable it, while Ubuntu generally does not and Amazon Linux limits it to smaller virtual machines.</span><span class='zh'>在运行并行度越来越高的 AI 工作负载时遇到内存不足后，作者转而使用 zram。zram 会压缩内存，Windows、macOS 和许多 Android 设备已经在使用这项技术，但不少 Linux 发行版默认未启用。Fedora 和 Pop!_OS 默认启用 zram，而 Ubuntu 通常没有启用，Amazon Linux 则只在较小的虚拟机上启用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.cornell.edu/stories/2026/10/sheep-grazing-solar-fields-offers-farmers-economic-ecological-gains" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Grazing sheep in fields with solar arrays</span><span class='zh'>在太阳能阵列田地放牧绵羊</span></a><span class="dom">news.cornell.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Two studies examine the growing practice of grazing sheep beneath solar arrays.</span><span class='zh'>两项研究考察了在太阳能阵列下放牧绵羊这一日益普及的做法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Two studies examine the growing practice of grazing sheep beneath solar arrays. One found that grazing at two sheep per hectare increased wild bees, honey bees, and lady beetles by 38% compared with mowing, driven mainly by an 83% rise in honey bees. The other study describes a decision-support tool to help farmers expand solar-grazing herds profitably.</span><span class='zh'>两项研究考察了在太阳能阵列下放牧绵羊这一日益普及的做法。其中一项发现，每公顷放牧两只绵羊时，野生蜜蜂、蜜蜂和瓢虫的数量比割草对照地增加 38%，主要原因是蜜蜂数量增加了 83%。另一项研究介绍了一种决策支持工具，帮助农民以盈利方式扩大太阳能牧羊规模。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://cekrem.github.io/posts/if-you-stop-writing-code-you-also-get-worse-at-reviewing-it/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If you stop writing code, you also get worse at reviewing it</span><span class='zh'>停止写代码，也会让你的代码审查能力变差</span></a><span class="dom">cekrem.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that software engineers, like practitioners in other fields, need hands-on practice to maintain and improve their skills.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，软件工程师和其他领域的从业者一样，需要通过亲自实践来保持并提升技能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that software engineers, like practitioners in other fields, need hands-on practice to maintain and improve their skills. Reading about a skill is not equivalent to performing it, whether the skill involves technique, problem-solving, or muscle memory. The post questions why this principle is often treated differently in software engineering.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，软件工程师和其他领域的从业者一样，需要通过亲自实践来保持并提升技能。无论涉及技巧、解决问题还是肌肉记忆，阅读相关内容都不能替代实际操作。文章质疑了为什么这一原则在软件工程中常被区别对待。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tristanbuckmaster/navier-stokes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Finite-time blow-up for the Navier–Stokes equations</span><span class='zh'>Navier–Stokes 方程的有限时间爆破解</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A repository contains the paper “Finite-time blow-up for the Navier–Stokes equations” by L.</span><span class='zh'>一个代码仓库收录了 L. Alpöge 和 T. Buckmaster 撰写的《Navier–Stokes 方程的有限时间爆破解》，以及配套的 Lean 4 形式化证明。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A repository contains the paper “Finite-time blow-up for the Navier–Stokes equations” by L. Alpöge and T. Buckmaster, together with an accompanying Lean 4 formalization. The formalization proves the stated theorems from a library while restricting the permitted axioms to propext, Quot.sound, and Classical.choice. Comparator files check that the theorem statements match the solutions.</span><span class='zh'>一个代码仓库收录了 L. Alpöge 和 T. Buckmaster 撰写的《Navier–Stokes 方程的有限时间爆破解》，以及配套的 Lean 4 形式化证明。该形式化代码通过一个库证明所述定理，并将允许使用的公理限制为 propext、Quot.sound 和 Classical.choice。比较文件用于检查定理陈述与解答是否一致。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gulivan/worker755" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I built a game where you are an LLM&#x27;s subagent</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我做了一个让你成为大语言模型子代理的游戏</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Worker755 is a browser game in which an AI manager treats the player as its subagent.</span><span class='zh'>Worker755 是一款浏览器游戏，玩家在其中扮演一个被 AI 管理器视为子代理的角色。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Worker755 is a browser game in which an AI manager treats the player as its subagent. The manager is a real LLM, such as Claude, GPT, Gemini, DeepSeek, GLM, or Kimi, with a task queue, read-only machine access, and a tool for spawning subagents. Players receive instructions in a terminal, have 90 seconds to act, and then receive feedback, corrections, incident reports, and a performance review.</span><span class='zh'>Worker755 是一款浏览器游戏，玩家在其中扮演一个被 AI 管理器视为子代理的角色。管理器使用真实的大语言模型，例如 Claude、GPT、Gemini、DeepSeek、GLM 或 Kimi，并配有任务队列、只读的机器访问权限和生成子代理的工具。玩家会在终端收到指令，并有 90 秒执行；随后系统会反馈结果、提出修正、提交事故报告，最后给出绩效评估。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pithings/unkernel" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A real Linux kernel, compiled to WebAssembly</span><span class='zh'>真正的 Linux 内核，编译为 WebAssembly</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Unkernel compiles a real Linux kernel to WebAssembly.</span><span class='zh'>Unkernel 将真正的 Linux 内核编译成 WebAssembly。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Unkernel compiles a real Linux kernel to WebAssembly. The kernel boots to a shell in less than one second. The project also provides performance information, feature documentation, benchmarks, and a JavaScript SDK.</span><span class='zh'>Unkernel 将真正的 Linux 内核编译成 WebAssembly。该内核启动到 shell 所需时间不到一秒。项目还提供性能信息、功能文档、基准测试和 JavaScript SDK。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/EL4CTEO/fast-anything" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fast-Anything: Don&#x27;t rewrite it in Rust. Rewrite the 5% that&#x27;s slow</span><span class='zh'>Fast-Anything：别用 Rust 重写全部，只重写最慢的 5%</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fast-Anything profiles Python, Node, or Ruby applications, ports their hottest functions with a coding agent, and verifies that behavior remains unchanged.</span><span class='zh'>Fast-Anything 会分析 Python、Node 或 Ruby 应用的性能热点，用编程代理移植最慢的函数，并验证行为是否保持一致。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fast-Anything profiles Python, Node, or Ruby applications, ports their hottest functions with a coding agent, and verifies that behavior remains unchanged. Its premise is that rewriting an entire application in Rust can take months, while most runtime is concentrated in a small number of functions. The tool therefore targets only the slowest 5% rather than replacing the whole application.</span><span class='zh'>Fast-Anything 会分析 Python、Node 或 Ruby 应用的性能热点，用编程代理移植最慢的函数，并验证行为是否保持一致。它的出发点是，整个应用用 Rust 重写可能耗时数月，而大部分运行时间通常集中在少数函数上。因此，该工具只针对最慢的 5%，而不是替换整个应用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/sebaandrada-cell/PcapAnalyzer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PcapAnalyzer – self-hosted pcap analyzer with 27 problem detectors</span><span class='zh'>PcapAnalyzer：带 27 种问题检测器的自托管 pcap 分析器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PcapAnalyzer analyzes .pcap, .pcapng, and gzip-compressed captures and identifies what is failing, why, and what to check next.</span><span class='zh'>PcapAnalyzer 可分析 .pcap、.pcapng 以及 gzip 压缩的抓包文件，并指出故障所在、原因和后续检查方向。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PcapAnalyzer analyzes .pcap, .pcapng, and gzip-compressed captures and identifies what is failing, why, and what to check next. It includes 27 problem detectors and AI-assisted investigation. The open-source Apache 2.0 tool runs locally, so captures do not leave the network, and it is currently in beta.</span><span class='zh'>PcapAnalyzer 可分析 .pcap、.pcapng 以及 gzip 压缩的抓包文件，并指出故障所在、原因和后续检查方向。它包含 27 种问题检测器，并支持 AI 辅助调查。这个采用 Apache 2.0 许可的开源工具在本地运行，因此抓包数据不会离开网络，目前仍处于测试阶段。</span></p>

The device supports FAT12 1.44 MB HD disks.</span><span class='zh'>Floppy3.5 是一款便携式 Wi‑Fi NAS，可读取包括 Sony Mavica 等老式数码相机使用的 3.5 英寸软盘。它能通过网页显示文件和照片，并将其复制到 microSD 卡。该设备支持 FAT12 1.44 MB 高密度软盘。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/onlook-dev/onlook/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Launch HN: Onlook (YC W25) – An open-source developer tool for designers</span><span class='zh'>Launch HN：Onlook（YC W25）——面向设计师的开源开发工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Onlook is an open-source desktop app that places a design canvas over a real codebase, allowing designers to work directly in code with an existing agent.</span><span class='zh'>Onlook 是一款开源桌面应用，在真实代码库之上提供设计画布，让设计师可以借助现有代理直接进行代码设计。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Onlook is an open-source desktop app that places a design canvas over a real codebase, allowing designers to work directly in code with an existing agent. Users can work in multiple simultaneous worktrees running the actual app locally, with changes submitted as reviewable or discardable pull requests. The project says more than 25,000 designers and developers have supported it and over 100 people have contributed.</span><span class='zh'>Onlook 是一款开源桌面应用，在真实代码库之上提供设计画布，让设计师可以借助现有代理直接进行代码设计。用户可以在多个同时运行真实应用的工作树中操作，并将每次修改提交为可审查或可丢弃的拉取请求。项目方称已有超过 25,000 名设计师和开发者支持该项目，贡献者超过 100 人。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/aryamandarda/tamserve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Open-source, real-time object tracking for live video feeds</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：面向实时视频流的开源实时目标跟踪</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tamserve runs Meta’s SAM 3 on live camera feeds, detecting named objects and tracking them across multiple cameras.</span><span class='zh'>Tamserve 在实时摄像头画面上运行 Meta 的 SAM 3，可根据名称发现目标，并在多个摄像头之间持续跟踪。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tamserve runs Meta’s SAM 3 on live camera feeds, detecting named objects and tracking them across multiple cameras. On an RTX 4090, it is reported to be four times faster than SAM 3’s tracker for arbitrary objects while retaining 92% of its accuracy; for people, it can handle 16 cameras concurrently. Its memory use stays around 3.5 GB on a 3,000-frame video, while SAM 3 runs out of memory at frame 423.</span><span class='zh'>Tamserve 在实时摄像头画面上运行 Meta 的 SAM 3，可根据名称发现目标，并在多个摄像头之间持续跟踪。在 RTX 4090 上，对于任意目标，其速度据称是 SAM 3 跟踪器的 4 倍，同时保持 92% 的准确率；针对人员，它可以同时处理 16 个摄像头。处理 3,000 帧视频时，其内存占用保持在约 3.5 GB，而 SAM 3 在第 423 帧就会耗尽内存。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>119</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50523377' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>13</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50581403" data-id="50581403" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Programming" data-score="118" data-comments="36" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50581403" data-time="1795205996">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/diggerhq/opencomputer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/diggerhq/opencomputer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OpenComputer – an open source alternative to Devin</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：OpenComputer——Devin 的开源替代品</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenComputer is an open-source cloud coding agent for teams that can run in a company’s AWS, GCP, or Azure account.</span><span class='zh'>OpenComputer 是一个面向团队的开源云端编码代理，可以运行在企业自己的 AWS、GCP 或 Azure 账户中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenComputer is an open-source cloud coding agent for teams that can run in a company’s AWS, GCP, or Azure account. Its agent harness and sandbox share a durable VM, while sessions are isolated in Git worktrees and backed up after every turn. The authors report a first commit time of 24 seconds, compared with 53–72 seconds for alternatives they benchmarked, and say a continuously running VM can become cheaper at higher usage.</span><span class='zh'>OpenComputer 是一个面向团队的开源云端编码代理，可以运行在企业自己的 AWS、GCP 或 Azure 账户中。它将代理工具链和沙箱运行在同一台持久化虚拟机上，使用 Git worktree 隔离会话，并在每轮操作后备份会话文件。作者报告称其首次提交耗时 24 秒，而对比方案为 53 至 72 秒；在较高使用量下，持续运行的虚拟机也可能更便宜。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>118</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50581403' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>36</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50496787" data-id="50496787" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Web|Data|Education" data-score="112" data-comments="68" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50496787" data-time="1794662230">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/ZigaoWang/zigzag" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1433164653/5ae486cd-f2b6-49e2-a788-7f730a3a9823' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ZigaoWang/zigzag" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Zigzag, a native Mac browser for people leaving Arc</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Zigzag——为离开 Arc 的用户打造的原生 Mac 浏览器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zigzag is a free, open-source Mac browser written in Swift and based on WebKit, created by a high-school student from Shanghai.</span><span class='zh'>Zigzag 是一款由上海高中生开发的免费开源 Mac 浏览器，使用 Swift 编写并基于 WebKit。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zigzag is a free, open-source Mac browser written in Swift and based on WebKit, created by a high-school student from Shanghai. It aims to provide an Arc-like interface and can import spaces, pins, history, passwords, and cookies from Arc or other browsers. Despite using WebKit, it supports Chrome extensions, including 1Password, Bitwarden, and Tampermonkey. The browser requires no account and claims to have no telemetry or analytics.</span><span class='zh'>Zigzag 是一款由上海高中生开发的免费开源 Mac 浏览器，使用 Swift 编写并基于 WebKit。它试图提供类似 Arc 的界面，并可从 Arc 或其他浏览器导入空间、固定项目、历史记录、密码和 Cookie。虽然基于 WebKit，它仍支持 Chrome 扩展，包括 1Password、Bitwarden 和 Tampermonkey。该浏览器无需账户，并声称不包含遥测或分析功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/eclipse-mosquitto/mosquitto" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto – An open source MQTT broker</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto：开源 MQTT 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto is an open-source implementation of an MQTT broker supporting MQTT versions 5.0, 3.1.1, and 3.1.</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto 是一个开源 MQTT 代理实现，支持 MQTT 5.0、3.1.1 和 3.1 版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto is an open-source implementation of an MQTT broker supporting MQTT versions 5.0, 3.1.1, and 3.1. It also provides C and C++ client libraries and command-line tools for publishing, subscribing, and administering the broker.</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto 是一个开源 MQTT 代理实现，支持 MQTT 5.0、3.1.1 和 3.1 版本。它还提供 C 和 C++ 客户端库，以及用于发布、订阅和管理代理的命令行工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ages2001/WINUDF" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Winudf – DVD / BluRay driver for DOS, Windows 1.x – 3x / 95 / 98 / ME</span><span class='zh'>Winudf：适用于 DOS、Windows 1.x–3.x、95、98、ME 的 DVD/Blu-ray 驱动</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WINUDF is an installable file-system driver that lets DOS and early Windows systems read and write UDF media.</span><span class='zh'>WINUDF 是一种可安装文件系统驱动，让 DOS 和早期 Windows 系统能够读写 UDF 媒体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WINUDF is an installable file-system driver that lets DOS and early Windows systems read and write UDF media. It supports UDF versions 1.02 through 2.60, including DVDs, Blu-ray discs, packet-written CDs, and UDF-formatted USB drives. DOS and Windows 1.0x–3.11 receive drive letters through WINUDF.EXE, while WINUDF.VXD provides a long-name file-system driver for Windows 95, 98, and ME.</span><span class='zh'>WINUDF 是一种可安装文件系统驱动，让 DOS 和早期 Windows 系统能够读写 UDF 媒体。它支持 1.02 至 2.60 版 UDF，涵盖 DVD、蓝光光盘、包写入 CD 以及采用 UDF 格式的 USB 驱动器。DOS 和 Windows 1.0x–3.11 可通过 WINUDF.EXE 获得盘符，Windows 95、98 和 ME 则使用支持长文件名的 WINUDF.VXD 驱动。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tamnd/riemann-handbook" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: The Riemann Proof Handbook</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：《黎曼证明手册》</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Riemann Handbook presents the Lean proof of the Riemann Hypothesis from the tamnd/riemann-lean project in mathematical prose, with definitions, theorems, lemmas, and proofs.</span><span class='zh'>《黎曼证明手册》将 tamnd/riemann-lean 项目中的黎曼猜想 Lean 证明，以包含定义、定理、引理和证明的数学文字形式呈现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Riemann Handbook presents the Lean proof of the Riemann Hypothesis from the tamnd/riemann-lean project in mathematical prose, with definitions, theorems, lemmas, and proofs. It is available as a PDF and EPUB book. The claim has not been checked: the full Lean project has not been built, and the written statements have not been verified against the code.</span><span class='zh'>《黎曼证明手册》将 tamnd/riemann-lean 项目中的黎曼猜想 Lean 证明，以包含定义、定理、引理和证明的数学文字形式呈现。该书提供 PDF 和 EPUB 版本。但其中的数学主张尚未经过验证：完整 Lean 项目还没有构建成功，书面陈述也未与代码逐一核对。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Apakottur/unref" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Unref – A Python dead code finder built on Ruff&#x27;s parser</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Unref——基于 Ruff 解析器的 Python 死代码查找器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Unref is a Python dead-code finder built with Rust and Ruff&#x27;s parser, created because existing alternatives did not work reliably on the author&#x27;s codebases.</span><span class='zh'>Unref 是一款使用 Rust 和 Ruff 解析器构建的 Python 死代码查找器，起因是现有替代工具无法可靠适配作者的代码库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Unref is a Python dead-code finder built with Rust and Ruff&#x27;s parser, created because existing alternatives did not work reliably on the author&#x27;s codebases. It can scan all of CPython in under a second and has found substantial dead code in the author&#x27;s team&#x27;s projects. The tool is intended for low-configuration use and is already being used in continuous integration.</span><span class='zh'>Unref 是一款使用 Rust 和 Ruff 解析器构建的 Python 死代码查找器，起因是现有替代工具无法可靠适配作者的代码库。它能在不到一秒内检查完整的 CPython，并在作者团队的项目中发现了大量死代码。该工具定位于低配置使用，目前已用于持续集成。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tom7/noperts/blob/main/septopert.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Smallest possible nopert polyhedra: the Septopert</span><span class='zh'>最小的非鲁珀特多面体：Septopert</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Septopert is a convex seven-vertex polyhedron that is not Rupert, meaning it cannot pass through a congruent copy of itself.</span><span class='zh'>Septopert 是一种拥有 7 个顶点、且不是鲁珀特多面体的凸多面体，也就是说它无法穿过与自身全等的另一个多面体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Septopert is a convex seven-vertex polyhedron that is not Rupert, meaning it cannot pass through a congruent copy of itself. Seven vertices is the minimum possible for a non-Rupert polyhedron. The page gives exact statements, Lean-checked proofs, and reproduction instructions for the canonical Septopert.</span><span class='zh'>Septopert 是一种拥有 7 个顶点、且不是鲁珀特多面体的凸多面体，也就是说它无法穿过与自身全等的另一个多面体。7 个顶点是非鲁珀特多面体可能达到的最少数量。页面提供了规范 Septopert 的精确陈述、经 Lean 检查的证明和复现步骤。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/XITRIX/ppsspp-nx2/blob/master/SWITCH2-GAMESHARE.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Running two PSP emulators on one Switch 2 and sharing one via GameShare</span><span class='zh'>在一台 Switch 2 上运行两个 PSP 模拟器，并通过 GameShare 共享一个</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A branch of the PPSSPP emulator for Switch 2 experiments with running a separate PSP guest on the host console and streaming it to a stock GameShare client.</span><span class='zh'>Switch 2 版 PPSSPP 模拟器的一个分支尝试在主机上运行独立的 PSP 客体，并将其串流到普通 GameShare 客户端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A branch of the PPSSPP emulator for Switch 2 experiments with running a separate PSP guest on the host console and streaming it to a stock GameShare client. The setup is not a mirrored controller or a second PSP controller in the host emulator. Compilation and host tests have not yet established console operation or acceptable two-game performance.</span><span class='zh'>Switch 2 版 PPSSPP 模拟器的一个分支尝试在主机上运行独立的 PSP 客体，并将其串流到普通 GameShare 客户端。该方案并不是镜像控制器，也不是在主模拟器中增加第二个 PSP 控制器。编译和主机测试尚未证明它能在游戏机上运行，也未证明双游戏性能达到可接受水平。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kborer/cells/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: An ASCII cell aquarium for your terminal</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：终端里的 ASCII 细胞水族箱</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This terminal program presents a virtual microscope slide rendered in ASCII characters.</span><span class='zh'>这个终端程序用 ASCII 字符呈现一个虚拟显微镜载玻片。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This terminal program presents a virtual microscope slide rendered in ASCII characters. Cells divide, immune cells chase bacteria, and bacteriophages occasionally create disruptions. It can be installed with pipx and run using the `cells` command.</span><span class='zh'>这个终端程序用 ASCII 字符呈现一个虚拟显微镜载玻片。细胞会分裂，免疫细胞会追逐细菌，噬菌体也会不时制造混乱。程序可通过 pipx 安装，并使用 `cells` 命令运行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RunanywhereAI/trnrt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: TrnRT – An open-source LLM inference engine for AWS Trainium</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：TrnRT——面向 AWS Trainium 的开源 LLM 推理引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RunAnywhere open-sourced TrnRT, an inference engine for AWS Trainium that currently serves GLM-5.3 Flash in FP8 on an AWS trn2.48xlarge instance.</span><span class='zh'>RunAnywhere 开源了 TrnRT，这是一个面向 AWS Trainium 的推理引擎，目前可在 AWS trn2.48xlarge 实例上以 FP8 运行 GLM-5.3 Flash。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RunAnywhere open-sourced TrnRT, an inference engine for AWS Trainium that currently serves GLM-5.3 Flash in FP8 on an AWS trn2.48xlarge instance. It can serve two model instances on one box using eight Trainium2 chips and a cache-aware router. On the SemiAnalysis AgentX benchmark, the team reports 3.57 million tokens per minute. The project targets large models with long context windows and aims to improve cost efficiency compared with H100 and H200 deployments.</span><span class='zh'>RunAnywhere 开源了 TrnRT，这是一个面向 AWS Trainium 的推理引擎，目前可在 AWS trn2.48xlarge 实例上以 FP8 运行 GLM-5.3 Flash。它可借助 8 个 Trainium2 芯片和感知缓存的路由器，在一台机器上服务两个模型实例。团队称，其在 SemiAnalysis AgentX 基准上的性能达到每分钟 357 万个 token。该项目面向长上下文大模型，目标是相较 H100 和 H200 部署提升成本效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/plabayo/venndb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: VennDB: append-only in-memory Rust DB for rows queried using bit flags</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：VennDB——用位标志查询行数据的追加式内存 Rust 数据库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>VennDB is an append-only, in-memory database written in Rust that queries rows through bit-flag columns.</span><span class='zh'>VennDB 是一个用 Rust 编写的追加式内存数据库，通过位标志列查询行数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>VennDB is an append-only, in-memory database written in Rust that queries rows through bit-flag columns. It targets mostly static datasets loaded at startup and queried frequently with simple filters, with an emphasis on speed and compactness. For its limited use cases, the project has few dependencies and is described as faster than a naive implementation or a heavier option such as SQLite.</span><span class='zh'>VennDB 是一个用 Rust 编写的追加式内存数据库，通过位标志列查询行数据。它面向主要在启动时加载、之后频繁使用简单过滤条件查询的静态数据集，重点是速度和紧凑性。在适用范围有限的场景中，该项目依赖很少，并被描述为比朴素实现或 SQLite 等更重量级方案更快。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/n4ze3m/tiny-voca" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A 4.5M-parameter TTS model runs 70x real-time on CPU and cost just $10 to train</span><span class='zh'>一款 450 万参数的 TTS 模型在 CPU 上以实时速度 70 倍运行，训练成本仅 10 美元</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tiny-voca is a small English text-to-speech model with two voices, F2 and M2.</span><span class='zh'>Tiny-voca 是一款支持 F2 和 M2 两种声音的轻量级英语文本转语音模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tiny-voca is a small English text-to-speech model with two voices, F2 and M2. Its complete model, including the waveform generator, has 4.5 million parameters and was trained from scratch on about 18 hours of synthetic speech for roughly $10. On four CPU threads, it runs at about 70 times real time.</span><span class='zh'>Tiny-voca 是一款支持 F2 和 M2 两种声音的轻量级英语文本转语音模型。包含波形生成器在内的完整模型有 450 万个参数，使用约 18 小时合成语音从头训练，成本约为 10 美元。在四个 CPU 线程上，它的运行速度约为实时速度的 70 倍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/0nelight/sutra-os" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Sutra-OS – A self-hosted ontology platform</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Sutra-OS——自托管本体平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sutra-OS is an open-source, self-hosted ontology platform.</span><span class='zh'>Sutra-OS 是一个开源、自托管的本体平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sutra-OS is an open-source, self-hosted ontology platform. It represents data as one owned model with typed and linked entities, server-enforced rules, and numbers traceable to their sources. The project describes its design as “Scarce Units, Typed, Related, Actionable.”</span><span class='zh'>Sutra-OS 是一个开源、自托管的本体平台。它将数据表示为用户拥有的统一模型，其中实体具有类型和关联，修改由服务器规则强制执行，所有数字都能追溯到来源。项目将其设计概括为“稀缺、类型化、关联、可行动”。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ThrudTheBarbarian/xc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Compile the same source for GPUs and CPUs</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：为 GPU 和 CPU 编译同一份源代码</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The xc language and xcc compiler turn ordinary loops into both multicore CPU code and GPU kernels, targeting formats including MSL, PTX, SPIR-V, and WGSL without requiring an SDK.</span><span class='zh'>xc 语言及其 xcc 编译器会将普通循环编译为多核 CPU 代码和 GPU 内核，支持 MSL、PTX、SPIR-V 和 WGSL 等格式，且无需安装 SDK。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The xc language and xcc compiler turn ordinary loops into both multicore CPU code and GPU kernels, targeting formats including MSL, PTX, SPIR-V, and WGSL without requiring an SDK. At runtime, programs benchmark each block on the machine and keep the faster placement. Version 1.0.1 supports Linux, macOS, and Windows on x86-64 and Arm64, and the compiler can also process plain C loops through Clang’s front end.</span><span class='zh'>xc 语言及其 xcc 编译器会将普通循环编译为多核 CPU 代码和 GPU 内核，支持 MSL、PTX、SPIR-V 和 WGSL 等格式，且无需安装 SDK。程序运行时会在当前机器上测试每个代码块，并选择更快的执行位置。1.0.1 版本支持 Linux、macOS 和 Windows 上的 x86-64 与 Arm64，编译器还可以通过 Clang 前端处理普通 C 循环。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>25</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50487155' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>4</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50604540" data-id="50604540" data-tags="Open Source|Programming" data-score="23" data-comments="5" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50604540" data-time="1795385928">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/OpenTodoApp/OpenTodo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OpenTodo – a self-hosted, local-first task manager in one Go binary</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：OpenTodo——单个 Go 二进制文件实现的自托管、本地优先任务管理器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenTodo is a free, open-source task manager that can be self-hosted or hosted for users.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTodo 是一款免费开源的任务管理器，用户可以自行部署或交由他人托管。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenTodo is a free, open-source task manager that can be self-hosted or hosted for users. It works on phones and computers, supports offline use and shared lists, and keeps users’ data under their control. The project is designed as a local-first alternative to subscription-based task applications.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTodo 是一款免费开源的任务管理器，用户可以自行部署或交由他人托管。它支持手机和电脑、离线使用以及与家人或团队共享列表，并让用户掌控自己的数据。该项目旨在提供一种本地优先的替代方案，减少对订阅制任务应用的依赖。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Ymsniper/vault-share" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A live service duress prototype</span><span class='zh'>实时服务型胁迫原型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Vault-share is a self-hosted, invite-only encrypted file-sharing application built with Flask.</span><span class='zh'>Vault-share 是一个基于 Flask 的自托管、仅限邀请的加密文件共享应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Vault-share is a self-hosted, invite-only encrypted file-sharing application built with Flask. Each file uses its own AES-256-GCM key, which is sealed separately for every authorized reader using HPKE, while uploads are signed and integrity-checked. The prototype also supports duress passwords that reveal separate cover vaults and can require a USB flash drive for the master key.</span><span class='zh'>Vault-share 是一个基于 Flask 的自托管、仅限邀请的加密文件共享应用。每个文件使用独立的 AES-256-GCM 密钥，并通过 HPKE 分别封装给每位获授权的读取者；上传内容还会进行签名和完整性校验。该原型还支持在胁迫密码下打开独立的伪装保险库，并可要求使用 USB 闪存盘保存主密钥。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>14</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50509980' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50589235" data-id="50589235" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Open Source" data-score="12" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50589235" data-time="1795269188">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/natsu0529/agent-mcts" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: agent-mcts – Monte Carlo tree search on top of Claude Code</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：agent-mcts——在 Claude Code 之上运行蒙特卡洛树搜索</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>agent-mcts is an open-source MIT-licensed harness that applies Monte Carlo Tree Search to coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex CLI.</span><span class='zh'>agent-mcts 是一个采用 MIT 许可的开源工具，可将蒙特卡洛树搜索应用于 Claude Code 和 Codex CLI 等编程代理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>agent-mcts is an open-source MIT-licensed harness that applies Monte Carlo Tree Search to coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex CLI. Instead of following one solution path, it explores multiple branches, backtracks from dead ends, and directs more budget toward promising paths. The project has been verified with Claude Code and lists support for other agents.</span><span class='zh'>agent-mcts 是一个采用 MIT 许可的开源工具，可将蒙特卡洛树搜索应用于 Claude Code 和 Codex CLI 等编程代理。它不再只沿着一条方案路径前进，而是探索多个分支、从死路回退，并将更多预算投入到有希望的路径上。该项目已通过 Claude Code 验证，并列出了对其他代理的支持情况。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>12</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50589235' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50550829" data-id="50550829" data-tags="Programming|AI" data-score="9" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50550829" data-time="1795029875">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Viktotovich/vb-harness-for-agentic-building" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>VB Harness – Make local coding agents verify their own work</span><span class='zh'>VB Harness——让本地编程智能体自行验证工作</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>VB Harness makes local coding agents work in small tasks and isolated copies, using a project’s own checks to validate each change.</span><span class='zh'>VB Harness 将本地编程智能体的工作拆分为小任务，并在隔离副本中执行，通过项目自身的检查来验证每次修改。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>VB Harness makes local coding agents work in small tasks and isolated copies, using a project’s own checks to validate each change. It supports LM Studio, Ollama, and other OpenAI-compatible models, and retains changes only when type checks, linting, builds, tests, and independent review succeed. The tool is designed to make local agents accountable without trying to make the underlying model smarter.</span><span class='zh'>VB Harness 将本地编程智能体的工作拆分为小任务，并在隔离副本中执行，通过项目自身的检查来验证每次修改。它支持 LM Studio、Ollama 和其他兼容 OpenAI API 的模型，只有在类型检查、代码检查、构建、测试和独立复审均通过后才会保留改动。该工具旨在让本地智能体对结果负责，而不是试图直接提升底层模型的智能程度。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>9</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50550829' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50554941" data-id="50554941" data-tags="DevOps|Programming" data-score="4" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50554941" data-time="1795051843">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/Ri4dH/failtrace" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#14b8a6' data-tag='DevOps'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Ri4dH/failtrace" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FailTrace – Reproduce a failed GitHub Actions run locally from its URL</span><span class='zh'>FailTrace：通过 URL 在本地复现失败的 GitHub Actions 运行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>FailTrace takes the URL of a failed GitHub Actions run and identifies the failed job and step.</span><span class='zh'>FailTrace 接收失败的 GitHub Actions 运行 URL，并找出失败的作业和步骤。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>FailTrace takes the URL of a failed GitHub Actions run and identifies the failed job and step. It matches that failure to the workflow file at the exact commit, extracts the relevant log output, and explains what is needed to reproduce the issue locally. The tool is designed to reduce guesswork when debugging CI failures.</span><span class='zh'>FailTrace 接收失败的 GitHub Actions 运行 URL，并找出失败的作业和步骤。它会将失败过程与对应提交中的工作流文件匹配，提取相关日志，并说明在本地复现问题所需的条件。该工具旨在减少调试 CI 失败时的猜测。</span></p>
